1	---
2	name: Porting epic and workflow
3	description: SPI-238 epic for porting dev_sprint to dev — 13 tickets, 5-phase agent workflow, parallel lanes, dependency order
4	type: project
5	---
6	
7	Epic SPI-238 tracks porting all capability slices from dev_sprint to dev in dependency order.
8	
9	**Why:** dev_sprint has ~500 files of feature work (FS1-FS12, storage, validation, QC, LLM routing, API) that needs to land on dev as reviewable, tested PRs — not a raw merge.
10	
11	**How to apply:**
12	- 13 sub-tickets (SPI-225 through SPI-237), each one port slice
13	- SPI-225 (storage) and SPI-226 (prompt/LLM) already landed
14	- SPI-227 (document storage) in progress
15	- 4 parallel lanes possible; see epic description for dependency graph
16	- Every ticket follows a 5-phase workflow: Research → Harmonize → Ask questions (BLOCKING) → Implement → Open PR
17	- Phase 2 harmonization findings go as a comment on the Linear ticket for discussion
18	- Phase 3 is a hard gate — no implementation without explicit user approval
19	- PRs reference the SPI ticket number in the title
20	- Don't move to dependent tickets until blocking PRs are merged on dev
21	- No need to pin dev_sprint commit — use whatever is current at time of work
22	- Tickets with dedicated test plans (FS5, FS6, FS7) must follow them; others must propose test approach for approval
23	- Status vocabulary from AGENTS.md: pending, in_progress, implemented (code exists but not verified), verified (all gates passed)
24	
```

-SQLAlchemy models and helpers for database-backed storage.

-Schema: "The Paddy Schema" — 18 tables with class table inheritance,
-real FK constraints, PostgreSQL ENUMs, and proper metric columns.
-See /matrix/Proposed-Schema.md for the full design document.
+Schema: "The Paddy Schema" with class table inheritance, real FK
+constraints, PostgreSQL ENUMs, and proper metric columns.
+See docs/plans/reference/proposed-database-schema.md for the design reference.
 """
 
 from __future__ import annotations
@@ -12,14 +11,17 @@ from collections.abc import Iterator
 from contextlib import contextmanager
 from datetime import UTC, datetime
 from enum import Enum as PyEnum
+from pathlib import Path
 from typing import Any
 
 from sqlalchemy import (
     Boolean,
+    CheckConstraint,
     DateTime,
     Enum,
     Float,
     ForeignKey,
+    ForeignKeyConstraint,
     Index,
     Integer,
     SmallInteger,
@@ -34,11 +36,88 @@ from sqlalchemy.types import JSON, String
 
 from .runtime_config import StorageRuntimeConfig, resolve_storage_runtime_config
 
+_REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+_ALEMBIC_INI_PATH = _REPO_ROOT / "alembic.ini"
+_ALEMBIC_SCRIPT_PATH = _REPO_ROOT / "alembic"
+_MODERN_CORE_TABLES = frozenset(
+    {
+        "question_sets",
+        "questions",
+        "jobs",
+        "runs",
+        "run_configs",
+        "run_steps",
+        "graphs",
+        "nodes",
+        "edges",
+        "graph_summaries",
+        "artifacts",
+        "crawl_url_state",
+    }
+)
+_LEGACY_ONLY_TABLES = frozenset(
+    {"graph_store_graphs", "graph_store_nodes", "graph_store_edges", "question_catalog", "question_set_catalog"}
+)
+
+
+class StorageSchemaNotReady(RuntimeError):
+    """Raised when a PostgreSQL database is not at the expected Alembic revision."""
+
 
 def utc_now() -> datetime:
     return datetime.now(UTC)
 
 
+def _storage_alembic_config(*, database_url: str | None = None) -> Any:
+    from alembic.config import Config
+
+    config = Config(str(_ALEMBIC_INI_PATH) if _ALEMBIC_INI_PATH.exists() else None)
+    config.set_main_option("script_location", str(_ALEMBIC_SCRIPT_PATH))
+    if database_url:
+        config.set_main_option("sqlalchemy.url", database_url)
+    return config
+
+
+def _alembic_head_revisions() -> tuple[str, ...]:
+    from alembic.script import ScriptDirectory
+
+    script = ScriptDirectory.from_config(_storage_alembic_config())
+    heads = tuple(sorted(str(revision).strip() for revision in script.get_heads() if str(revision).strip()))
+    if not heads:
+        raise RuntimeError("No Alembic heads found for the storage schema.")
+    return heads
+
+
+def _current_alembic_revisions(engine: Engine) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+    inspector = inspect(engine)
+    if not inspector.has_table("alembic_version"):
+        return tuple()
+    with engine.begin() as conn:
+        rows = conn.execute(text("SELECT version_num FROM alembic_version ORDER BY version_num")).fetchall()
+    return tuple(sorted(str(row[0]).strip() for row in rows if row and str(row[0]).strip()))
+
+
+def _detect_bootstrap_revision(engine: Engine) -> str | None:
+    existing_tables = set(inspect(engine).get_table_names())
+    if not existing_tables or (_LEGACY_ONLY_TABLES & existing_tables):
+        return None
+    if _MODERN_CORE_TABLES.issubset(existing_tables):
+        return "20260423_01"
+    return None
+
+
+def _stamp_existing_postgres_schema(database_url: str, revision: str) -> None:
+    from alembic import command
+
+    command.stamp(_storage_alembic_config(database_url=database_url), revision)
+
+
+def _upgrade_postgres_schema(database_url: str) -> None:
+    from alembic import command
+
+    command.upgrade(_storage_alembic_config(database_url=database_url), "head")
```

"""
Run-level config loading and precedence utilities for CLI pipelines.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import tomllib
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Final

from core.settings import (
    DEFAULT_GRAPH_ID,
    GJOPEN_MAX_PAGES_DEFAULT,
    GJOPEN_PROMPT_REV,
    GJOPEN_START_URL,
    PROJECT_ROOT,
)

DEFAULT_RUN_PROFILE_PATH: Final[Path] = PROJECT_ROOT / "config" / "run_profile.toml"
DEFAULT_SYSTEM_CONFIG_PATH: Final[Path] = PROJECT_ROOT / "config" / "system_defaults.toml"


# Maps run_profile.toml [models] keys → runtime override keys.
# Each key corresponds to a task ID in llm_tasks.toml:
#   research_query_boost    → FS2A.research_query_boost
#   research_domain_profile → FS2A.research_domain_profile
#   chunk_map               → FS3.chunk_map
#   reduce                  → FS3.reduce

def build_run_config_execution_snapshot(stored_run_config) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: ANN001
    llm_config = dict(stored_run_config.llm_config)
    snapshot = dict(llm_config)
    snapshot["run_config_id"] = stored_run_config.run_config_id
    snapshot["run_config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
    snapshot["config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
    snapshot["run_config"] = dict(stored_run_config.config)
    snapshot["llm_config"] = llm_config
    return snapshot


def create_run_research_run_config(
    *,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig,
    cli_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
    label: str = "",
    description: str = "",
    source_kind: str = "manual",
    created_by: str | None = None,
):
    from packages.inference.llm import build_llm_config_snapshot
    from packages.storage.run_config_store import create_or_get_run_config

    behavior_overrides = {key: value for key, value in dict(cli_overrides).items() if key in RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS}
    settings, _paths = build_effective_run_research_settings(
        cli_overrides=behavior_overrides,
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )
    llm_config = build_llm_config_snapshot(model_set=str(settings.get("model_set") or "").strip() or None)
    settings["model_set"] = str(llm_config.get("model_set") or settings.get("model_set") or "default")
    return create_or_get_run_config(
        config=build_run_research_behavior_config(settings),
        llm_config=llm_config,
        label=label,
        description=description,
        source_kind=source_kind,
        created_by=created_by,
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )


def load_run_research_run_config(
    *,
    run_config_id: str,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig,
):
    from packages.storage.run_config_store import require_run_config

    return require_run_config(run_config_id=run_config_id, storage_config=storage_config)


def apply_run_config_to_settings(settings: Mapping[str, Any], stored_run_config) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: ANN001
    merged = dict(settings)
    for key in RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS:
        merged.pop(key, None)
    merged.update(run_research_settings_from_run_config(stored_run_config.config))
    merged["run_config_id"] = stored_run_config.run_config_id
    merged["run_config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
    merged["run_config_snapshot"] = build_run_config_execution_snapshot(stored_run_config)
    return merged


def _resolve_path_input(value: Path | None, *, default: Path) -> Path:
    path = value or default
    path = Path(path).expanduser()
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = (PROJECT_ROOT / path).resolve()
    return path


def _load_toml_payload(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        payload = tomllib.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: PERF203
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to parse TOML config at {path}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):

---

## Phase 2: Harmonization — SPI-236

**Source artifacts:** SPI-236 ticket description, 8 design docs (orchestration + FS12), state-transition conformance report, capability diff audit.

---

### File inventory by action

**New files to create (3 in scope, 1 deferred):**

| File | Action | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `services/api/auth.py` | Create | Multi-user auth: `User`, `UserRegistry`, TOML loading, `get_current_user` dependency |
| `services/worker/run_config_context.py` | Create | Worker-side run-config cascade resolution helpers |
| `tests/test_api_auth.py` | Create | 500-line test suite for auth module |
| `services/api/routes/fs12.py` | **DEFER** | Imports `packages.domain.fs12` which doesn't exist on dev |

**Files with meaningful deltas to port (6):**

| File | Delta | Key changes |
|---|---|---|
| `packages/contracts/jobs.py` | +16 lines | `run_config_id` required on submit DTOs; `run_config_id`/`run_config_hash`/`model_set`/`run_config_snapshot` on `JobRequestContext`; `run_config_id`/`config_hash`/`llm_config` on `JobResultSummary` |
| `packages/storage/job_queue.py` | +49/-2 | `run_config_id` on `ClaimedJob`, `submit_job`, `complete_job` RunRow sync; `store_and_register_artifact` combo method; enriched `_ensure_run_row` |
| `services/api/job_service.py` | +80 lines | `_resolve_run_config_for_submission`, `ArtifactBytesMissingError`, run_config_id/model_set threading through all submit methods |
| `services/api/worker_dispatch.py` | +65/-14 | Load run_config from DB, build execution snapshot, wrap pipelines in `model_set_context` |
| `services/api/routes/jobs.py` | +34/-10 | Pass `run_config_id` to service, catch `ValueError` for invalid configs |
| `services/api/app.py` (shared) | +23/-10 | Load `user_registry` onto app state, log multi-user/fallback/disabled modes |

**Shared file — partial scope (1):**

| File | In scope | Out of scope |
|---|---|---|
| `services/api/mappers.py` | `worker_payload_to_job_result` run_config/llm_config fields (+12 lines) | `target_set_integrity` / `assert_target_set_integrity_passes` (FS1) |

**Files identical on both branches (no port needed):**
- `services/api/config.py`, `services/api/dependencies.py`, `packages/storage/runtime_config.py`, `tests/test_job_queue_artifacts.py`, `tests/test_run_detail_api.py`

---

### Dependency gap: `core/run_config.py`

This is NOT listed as an owned file, but **3 functions that only exist on dev_sprint** are imported by owned files:

- `create_run_research_run_config` — needed by `job_service.py`
- `load_run_research_run_config` — needed by `job_service.py` + `worker_dispatch.py`
- `build_run_config_execution_snapshot` — needed by `worker_dispatch.py`

These depend on `packages.storage.run_config_store` and `packages.inference.llm.build_llm_config_snapshot`, both of which **already exist on dev**. The `core/run_config.py` diff is ~389 lines total, but the SPI-236-relevant additions are:
- 3 new functions (~60 lines)
- `RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS` constant (~35 lines)
- `storage_config` parameter additions to `build_effective_run_research_settings` (~10 lines)

**Proposal:** Port the minimal subset of `core/run_config.py` changes needed. Leave the `bootstrap_runtime_config_to_database`, `load_question_set_definitions` DB path, and `generate_questions` storage_config changes out — those are broader infrastructure scope.

---

### Contradictions and findings

1. **FS12 routes blocker:** `routes/fs12.py` imports from `packages.domain.fs12` which doesn't exist on dev. **Recommend deferring** fs12 router registration in `app.py`. The FS12 route file itself can be created but not wired.

2. **Auth dual-system (intentional):** dev_sprint keeps both `require_api_key` (old, in `dependencies.py`) and `get_current_user` (new, in `auth.py`). Routes still use the old system. The new module loads the registry onto `app.state` but doesn't enforce at route level. This is documented as "sub-PR 1 scope" — port as-is.

3. **Conformance report inaccuracy:** Report claims `job_queue.py` has "no diff vs origin/dev" — actual diff is +49/-2. The run_config_id plumbing was added after the report was written.

4. **mappers.py scope bleed:** The diff includes `target_set_integrity` from FS1 — exclude from this PR.

5. **driver_promotion.py edge type fix:** `.strip().upper()` comparison fix is a bug fix, not SPI-236 scope.

---

### Proposed port order

1. `core/run_config.py` — minimal DB-backed functions
2. `packages/contracts/jobs.py` — DTO field additions
3. `packages/storage/job_queue.py` — run_config_id plumbing
4. `services/api/auth.py` — new multi-user auth module
5. `services/api/job_service.py` — run-config resolution
6. `services/api/worker_dispatch.py` — run-config + model_set_context
7. `services/worker/run_config_context.py` — worker context helpers
8. `services/api/routes/jobs.py` — route-level run_config_id passthrough
9. `services/api/mappers.py` — ONLY run_config/llm_config result fields
10. `services/api/app.py` — user registry loading (defer fs12 router)
11. `tests/test_api_auth.py` — auth test suite

### Out of scope (deliberately)

- `services/api/routes/fs12.py` wiring — blocked on `packages/domain/fs12`
- `services/api/routes/driver_promotion.py` edge type fix
- `services/api/mappers.py` target_set_integrity
- `packages/storage/database.py` Alembic infrastructure
- Route-level auth enforcement with `get_current_user` (sub-PR 2+)
- `submitted_by` propagation to JobRow (sub-PR 4)
- `bootstrap_runtime_config_to_database` and broader `core/run_config.py` infra

### Proposed test plan

- Port `tests/test_api_auth.py` from dev_sprint (covers registry, TOML, fallback, validation, app wiring)
- Existing `tests/test_job_queue_artifacts.py` as regression (identical, already passes)
- Existing `tests/test_run_detail_api.py` as regression (identical, already passes)
- Baseline: `uv run python -m pytest --ignore=tests/frontend -x -q`
- Targeted: `uv run python -m pytest tests/test_api_auth.py -q`

---

**Phase 3 gate: I need your approval before implementing.** Key decisions for you:

1. **FS12 routes** — defer entirely, or create the file but skip wiring?
2. **`core/run_config.py` gap** — OK to port the minimal subset (~60 lines of new functions + constants), or should this go in a separate PR?
3. **Overall scope** — does this look right, or should anything be added/removed?

> DEVELOPER

Just describe to me the point of this and the deciddiosns that are being made?

> AGENT

**What SPI-236 is about:** The API on `dev` currently has bare-bones job submission and a single shared API key. This port brings over three capabilities from dev_sprint:

1. **Multi-user auth** — Instead of one `ELIJAH_API_KEY` for everyone, a TOML-based user registry where each user gets their own key and identity. Routes still use the old single-key check for now; the registry is loaded and ready but not enforced yet (intentional phased rollout).

2. **Run-config propagation** — When someone submits a job through the API, the run configuration (model set, behavior flags, etc.) now flows all the way through: API request → job queue row → worker dispatch → pipeline execution. Today the config is partially threaded; after this port, every job carries an immutable `run_config_id` that's content-addressed and DB-backed, so you can reproduce any run.

3. **Worker context helpers** — A small utility module that resolves which run config, model set, and LLM profile a worker should use, with a cascade of fallbacks (explicit args → snapshot → request context → profile defaults).

**Key decisions in the proposed approach:**

- **Deferring FS12 routes** — The ticket lists `routes/fs12.py` as owned, but it imports `packages.domain.fs12` which doesn't exist on dev. Can't wire routes to a missing domain package. Proposing to skip it here.

- **Porting a slice of `core/run_config.py`** — This file isn't listed as owned by SPI-236, but the service layer imports 3 functions from it that only exist on dev_sprint. Without them, job_service and worker_dispatch won't work. Proposing to port just those ~60 lines of DB-backed config functions rather than the full 389-line diff.

- **Scoping out tangential changes** — The dev_sprint branch has several changes in shared files (edge type bug fix in driver_promotion, FS1 target_set_integrity in mappers, Alembic infrastructure in database.py) that happened to land alongside SPI-236 work but aren't part of this feature. Proposing to leave those for their respective tickets.

- **Keeping auth dual-system as-is** — dev_sprint intentionally has both the old `require_api_key` and the new `get_current_user` coexisting. The new system is wired but not enforced at the route level yet. Porting it exactly as dev_sprint has it rather than trying to complete the migration.

Lane 4 has only one ticket — SPI-236. So the scope I described IS the full lane 4 scope. There's no second ticket after it.

Let me walk through what I think belongs to SPI-236 and flag where I'm unsure, so you can tell me if anything's wrong:

**Clearly in scope (owned files with real deltas or new):**
- `services/api/auth.py` — new, multi-user auth
- `services/api/job_service.py` — run-config resolution additions
- `services/api/worker_dispatch.py` — run-config + model_set propagation
- `services/api/routes/jobs.py` — pass run_config_id through
- `services/worker/run_config_context.py` — new, worker context helpers
- `packages/storage/job_queue.py` — run_config_id plumbing
- `packages/contracts/jobs.py` — DTO field additions
- `services/api/app.py` — user registry loading
- `services/api/mappers.py` — run_config fields in job result mapper
- `tests/test_api_auth.py` — new auth tests

**The two things I'm uncertain about:**

1. **`services/api/routes/fs12.py`** — The ticket lists it as owned. It exists on dev_sprint. But it imports `packages.domain.fs12` which doesn't exist on dev. Do I port the file anyway and just skip wiring it in `app.py`? Or do you want me to port it fully, which means I'd also need to pull in the FS12 domain package (which feels like it belongs to a different scope)?

2. **`core/run_config.py`** — Not listed as owned by SPI-236, but `job_service.py` and `worker_dispatch.py` both import 3 functions from it that only exist on dev_sprint. If I don't port those functions, the service layer breaks. Should I include them in this PR, or does this indicate a gap in an earlier ticket (SPI-225) that should be addressed separately?

Those are the only two loose ends. Everything else maps cleanly to SPI-236's owned file list. What do you want to do about those two?

> AGENT

No. Scanning all 13 tickets under SPI-238:

**`core/run_config.py` DB-backed functions** — Not owned by any ticket. SPI-225 ("base storage, migrations, and runtime config") was the closest fit and is already Done, but it didn't port these 3 functions. They fell through the cracks between SPI-225 and SPI-236.

**FS12 domain package (`packages/domain/fs12/`)** — There is no FS12 port ticket in the epic at all. The route file was assigned to SPI-236 but the domain code it depends on has no home.

So both are gaps in the epic. Your call on how to handle them:

1. **`core/run_config.py`** — I'd include the ~60 lines of needed functions in the SPI-236 PR since without them `job_service.py` and `worker_dispatch.py` literally won't import. Small, tightly coupled, makes sense to bundle.

2. **FS12 routes** — This is bigger. The domain package is a whole feature set (pricing, Kelly diagnostics, exposure caps). Options:
   - Create a new ticket for the FS12 domain + route port
   - Or just skip `routes/fs12.py` entirely from SPI-236 and note the gap

What do you want to do?

> DEVELOPER

Hello, could you just explain to me what was previously in the lane, what comes next in the lane, what this ticket does in the lane, and what's the whole goal of the lane.

> AGENT

Lane 4 is just this one ticket — SPI-236. There's nothing before it and nothing after it in the lane. It's a standalone lane.

**The whole goal of lane 4:** Get the API layer on `dev` to the point where it can accept jobs with proper user identity and carry run configuration all the way through to the worker that executes the pipeline. Right now on `dev`, the API can submit and poll jobs, but there's no multi-user auth and the run config doesn't propagate — it gets lost between submission and execution.

**What SPI-236 does:** It's the entire lane. It ports three things:
- Auth: who is making the request (multi-user registry instead of one shared key)
- Run-config propagation: what settings should this job use (model set, behavior flags), threaded from API request → DB → worker → pipeline
- Worker context: helpers so the worker knows how to resolve which config/model to use for a given job

**Why it's its own lane:** It doesn't depend on any of the feature-set ports (lanes 1 and 3) or the graph gate repair (lane 2). It only needed base storage (SPI-225) which already landed. So it can run in parallel with everything else.

**What depends on it:** Lane 5 (SPI-237, ops/Docker/seed) waits for all lanes to settle, but nothing else in the epic directly blocks on lane 4.

> DEVELOPER

there is only 4 lanes tho no??

> AGENT

You're right, my mistake. SPI-237 (ops/Docker/seed) is labeled "lane 5" in its ticket description but if you structured it as 4 lanes then it's probably just a tail cleanup step, not a real lane.

Either way, the point is the same — lane 4 is just SPI-236, one ticket, standalone. No predecessor, no successor within the lane. Ready to go since SPI-225 landed.

Want me to proceed with implementation? The two gaps I flagged (`core/run_config.py` functions and FS12 routes) still need your call.

> DEVELOPER

Ojay what does this ticket do again and also what are the loose ends we mentioned?

> AGENT

**What SPI-236 does:** Ports three capabilities from dev_sprint to dev:

1. **Multi-user auth** — Replaces the single shared `ELIJAH_API_KEY` with a TOML-based user registry where each user gets their own key and identity
2. **Run-config propagation** — When a job is submitted via the API, the run configuration (model set, behavior flags) now flows end-to-end: API request → job queue row → worker dispatch → pipeline execution. Currently it gets lost along the way.
3. **Worker context helpers** — Utility module so workers can resolve which config/model set to use for a given job

---

**The two loose ends:**

1. **`core/run_config.py`** — 3 functions (`create_run_research_run_config`, `load_run_research_run_config`, `build_run_config_execution_snapshot`) that only exist on dev_sprint. SPI-236's service layer imports them, so without them the code won't run. No other ticket covers them — they slipped through when SPI-225 (base storage) was completed. ~60 lines, tightly coupled to this work. My recommendation: include them in the SPI-236 PR.

2. **FS12 routes** — `services/api/routes/fs12.py` is listed as an owned file, but it imports `packages.domain.fs12` which doesn't exist on dev and no ticket in the epic covers porting the FS12 domain package. My recommendation: skip the FS12 route from this PR and note the gap.

> DEVELOPER

so you are sayin gthat some of the run config is missing, and also the fs12 is not on dev yet so we should not port that over yet

> AGENT

Exactly right. 

For the run config — the store layer and infra are on dev (SPI-225 landed that), but 3 higher-level functions that bridge the store to the API/worker layer were missed. They're small and directly needed by this ticket's code, so I'd include them here.

For FS12 — the route file is just a thin wrapper that calls into `packages/domain/fs12` which isn't on dev. No point wiring up routes to a domain package that doesn't exist yet. Skip it, note the gap.

Want me to go ahead and implement?

> DEVELOPER

Okay you must make a ticket in the epic that mentions that in this, we skipped fs12. There also must be an epic to port fs12, with the same ticket underneath it. Finally, mention in the review

110	    system_config_path: Path | None = None,

113	        run_profile_path=_resolve_path_input(run_profile_path, default=DEFAULT_RUN_PROFILE_PATH),

118	def build_effective_generate_questions_settings(
119	    *,
120	    cli_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
121	    run_profile_path: Path | None = None,
122	    system_config_path: Path | None = None,
123	) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], RunConfigPaths]:
124	    paths = resolve_run_config_paths(
125	        run_profile_path=run_profile_path,
126	        system_config_path=system_config_path,
127	    )
128	    file_overrides = load_generate_questions_file_overrides(paths=paths)
129	    merged = merge_generate_questions_settings(file_overrides=file_overrides, cli_overrides=cli_overrides)

148	def load_generate_questions_file_overrides(*, paths: RunConfigPaths) -> dict[str, Any]:
149	    merged: dict[str, Any] = {}
150	    system_payload = _load_toml_payload(paths.system_config_path)
151	    profile_payload = _load_toml_payload(paths.run_profile_path)
152	
153	    for source_name, payload in (
154	        ("system_defaults", system_payload),
155	        ("run_profile", profile_payload),
156	    ):
157	        if not payload:
158	            continue
159	        _reject_legacy_run_all(payload=payload, source_name=source_name)
160	        overrides = _extract_generate_questions_overrides(payload=payload, source_name=source_name)
161	        _apply_overrides(merged, overrides)
162	    return merged

168	    profile_payload = _load_toml_payload(paths.run_profile_path)

182	def load_question_set_definitions(*, paths: RunConfigPaths) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
183	    """
184	    Load and merge question-set definitions from system + profile configs.
185	
186	    Merge precedence:
187	    - system defaults first
188	    - run profile overrides same set IDs
189	    """
190	    merged: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
191	    system_payload = _load_toml_payload(paths.system_config_path)
192	    profile_payload = _load_toml_payload(paths.run_profile_path)
193	
194	    for source_name, payload in (
195	        ("system_defaults", system_payload),
196	        ("run_profile", profile_payload),
197	    ):
198	        if not payload:
199	            continue
200	        _reject_legacy_run_all(payload=payload, source_name=source_name)
201	        definitions = _extract_question_set_definitions(payload=payload, source_name=source_name)
202	        for set_id, definition in definitions.items():
203	            merged[set_id] = definition
204	    return merged

207	def merge_generate_questions_settings(
208	    *,
209	    file_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
210	    cli_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
211	) -> dict[str, Any]:
212	    merged = dict(GENERATE_QUESTIONS_DEFAULTS)
213	    _apply_overrides(merged, file_overrides)
214	    _apply_overrides(merged, cli_overrides)
215	    return merged
216	
217	
218	def merge_run_research_settings(
219	    *,
220	    file_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
221	    cli_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
222	) -> dict[str, Any]:
223	    merged = dict(RUN_RESEARCH_DEFAULTS)
224	    merged["model_overrides"] = dict(RUN_RESEARCH_DEFAULTS["model_overrides"])
225	    _apply_overrides(merged, file_overrides)
226	    _apply_overrides(merged, cli_overrides)
227	
228	    model_overrides = merged.get("model_overrides")
229	    if isinstance(model_overrides, Mapping):
230	        merged["model_overrides"] = {
231	            str(key): str(value).strip() for key, value in model_overrides.items() if str(key).strip() and str(value).strip()
232	        }
233	    else:
234	        merged["model_overrides"] = {}
235	
236	    # prefer-local can only be active when a local repo path is set.
237	    merged["prefer_local_repo"] = bool(merged.get("prefer_local_repo")) and merged.get("local_repo_path") is not None
238	    return merged

241	def serialise_effective_settings(
242	    *,
243	    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
244	    paths: RunConfigPaths,
245	) -> dict[str, Any]:
246	    payload: dict[str, Any] = {}
247	    for key, value in settings.items():
248	        if isinstance(value, Path):
249	            payload[key] = str(value)
250	        elif isinstance(value, list):
251	            payload[key] = [str(item) if isinstance(item, Path) else item for item in value]
252	        elif isinstance(value, Mapping):
253	            payload[key] = {
254	                str(sub_key): str(sub_value) if isinstance(sub_value, Path) else sub_value for sub_key, sub_value in value.items()
255	            }
256	        else:
257	            payload[key] = value
258	    payload["config_paths"] = {
259	        "run_profile_path": str(paths.run_profile_path),
260	        "system_config_path": str(paths.system_config_path),
261	    }
262	    return payload
263	
264	
265	def serialise_effective_generate_questions_settings(
266	    *,
267	    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
268	    paths: RunConfigPaths,
269	) -> dict[str, Any]:
270	    return serialise_effective_settings(settings=settings, paths=paths)
271	
272	
273	def serialise_effective_run_research_settings(
274	    *,
275	    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
276	    paths: RunConfigPaths,
277	) -> dict[str, Any]:
278	    return serialise_effective_settings(settings=settings, paths=paths)

280	
281	def _resolve_path_input(value: Path | None, *, default: Path) -> Path:
282	    path = value or default
283	    path = Path(path).expanduser()
284	    if not path.is_absolute():
285	        path = (PROJECT_ROOT / path).resolve()
286	    return path
287	
288	
289	def _load_toml_payload(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
290	    if not path.exists():
291	        return {}
292	    try:
293	        payload = tomllib.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
294	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: PERF203
295	        raise ValueError(f"Failed to parse TOML config at {path}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
296	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
297	        raise ValueError(f"Config file must contain a TOML table at root: {path}")
298	    return payload
299	
300	
301	def _reject_legacy_run_all(*, payload: Mapping[str, Any], source_name: str) -> None:
302	    if "run_all" in payload:
303	        raise ValueError(
304	            f"[{source_name}] legacy [run_all] config is no longer supported. Migrate to [generate_questions] and [run_research]."
305	        )
306	
307	
308	def _extract_generate_questions_overrides(*, payload: Mapping[str, Any], source_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
309	    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
310	    section = _mapping(payload.get("generate_questions"))
311	
312	    for key in ("start_url", "prompt_rev"):
313	        _assign_optional_str(section, key, out, source_name=source_name)
314	    for key in ("max_pages", "max_questions"):
315	        _assign_int(section, key, out, source_name=source_name)
316	    for key in (
317	        "overwrite_cache",
318	        "resume",
319	        "overwrite_existing",
320	        "retry_failed",
321	        "fail_loud",
322	        "prompt_for_missing_target_date",
323	        "prompt_for_domain_assignment",
324	    ):
325	        _assign_bool(section, key, out, source_name=source_name)
326	
327	    paths = _mapping(section.get("paths"))
328	    for key in ("output_path", "run_state_path"):
329	        _assign_path(paths, key, out, source_name=source_name)
330	
331	    return out
332	
333	
334	def _extract_question_set_definitions(*, payload: Mapping[str, Any], source_name: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
335	    out: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
336	    root = _mapping(payload.get("question_sets"))
337	    for raw_set_id, raw_definition in root.items():
338	        set_id = str(raw_set_id or "").strip()
339	        if not set_id:
340	            raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] question_sets keys must be non-empty")
341	        definition = _mapping(raw_definition)
342	        if not definition:
343	            raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] question_sets.{set_id} must be a table")
344	
345	        label = definition.get("label")
346	        if not isinstance(label, str) or not label.strip():
347	            raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] question_sets.{set_id}.label must be a non-empty string")
348	
349	        start_url = definition.get("start_url")
350	        if not isinstance(start_url, str) or not start_url.strip():
351	            raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] question_sets.{set_id}.start_url must be a non-empty string")
352	
353	        source = definition.get("source", _QUESTION_SET_SOURCE_GJOPEN)
354	        if not isinstance(source, str) or not source.strip():
355	            raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] question_sets.{set_id}.source must be a non-empty string")
356	        source_text = source.strip().lower()
357	        if source_text != _QUESTION_SET_SOURCE_GJOPEN:
358	            raise ValueError(
359	                f"[{source_name}] question_sets.{set_id}.source '{source_text}' is unsupported; "
360	                f"supported sources: {_QUESTION_SET_SOURCE_GJOPEN}"
361	            )
362	
363	        normalized: dict[str, Any] = {
364	            "id": set_id,
365	            "label": label.strip(),
366	            "source": source_text,
367	            "start_url": start_url.strip(),
368	        }
369	        for optional_key in ("prompt_rev", "graph_id"):
370	            value = definition.get(optional_key)
371	            if value is None:
372	                continue
373	            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
374	                raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] question_sets.{set_id}.{optional_key} must be a non-empty string when set")
375	            normalized[optional_key] = value.strip()
376	
377	        out[set_id] = normalized
378	    return out
379	
380	
381	def _extract_run_research_overrides(*, payload: Mapping[str, Any], source_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
382	    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
383	    section = _mapping(payload.get("run_research"))
384	
385	    if "question_ids" in section:
386	        raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] run_research.question_ids is not supported; pass --question-ids/--all at runtime")
387	
388	    _assign_optional_str(section, "graph_id", out, source_name=source_name)
389	    _assign_int(section, "limit", out, source_name=source_name)
390	
391	    paths = _mapping(section.get("paths"))
392	    for key in ("questions_path", "graphs_dir", "output_root", "local_repo_path"):
393	        _assign_path(paths, key, out, source_name=source_name)
394	
395	    research = _mapping(section.get("research"))
396	    for key in (
397	        "min_factors",
398	        "max_factors",
399	        "question_max_attempts",
400	        "question_summary_max_output_tokens",
401	        "indicator_max_per_driver",
402	    ):
403	        _assign_int(research, key, out, source_name=source_name)
404	    for key in (
405	        "clear_existing_pdfs",
406	        "use_llm_query_boost",
407	        "prefer_local_repo",
408	        "research_fail_loud",
409	        "include_debug_artifacts",
410	        "question_summary_enabled",
411	        "question_summary_llm_enabled",
412	        "qc_enabled",
413	        "qc_capture_prompts",
414	        "qc_include_chunk_text",
415	        "driver_indicator_enabled",
416	    ):
417	        _assign_bool(research, key, out, source_name=source_name)
418	    _assign_path(research, "local_repo_path", out, source_name=source_name)
419	
420	    budgets = _mapping(section.get("budgets"))
421	    for key in (
422	        "pdfs_per_scope",
423	        "feedthrough",
424	        "chunk_size",
425	        "max_online_pages",
426	        "native_max_chunk_contexts",
427	        "native_max_grounded_factors",
428	        "native_max_free_factors",
429	        "native_max_existing_candidates",
430	        "native_max_output_tokens",
431	        "driver_indicator_pdfs_per_driver",
432	        "driver_indicator_feedthrough",
433	        "driver_indicator_max_drivers",
434	        "indicator_max_per_driver",
435	    ):
436	        _assign_int(budgets, key, out, source_name=source_name)
437	
438	    models = _mapping(payload.get("models"))
439	    model_overrides: dict[str, str] = {}
440	    for key, runtime_key in _MODEL_KEY_MAP.items():
441	        if key not in models:
442	            continue
443	        value = models.get(key)
444	        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
445	            raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] models.{key} must be a non-empty string")
446	        model_overrides[runtime_key] = value.strip()
447	    if model_overrides:
448	        out["model_overrides"] = model_overrides
449	
450	    return out
451	
452	
453	def _mapping(value: object) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
454	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
455	        return value
456	    return {}
457	
458	
459	def _assign_optional_str(
460	    section: Mapping[str, Any],
461	    key: str,
462	    out: dict[str, Any],
463	    *,
464	    source_name: str,
465	) -> None:
466	    if key not in section:
467	        return
468	    value = section.get(key)
469	    if not isinstance(value, str):
470	        raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] {key} must be a string")
471	    out[key] = value.strip()
472	
473	
474	def _assign_int(
475	    section: Mapping[str, Any],
476	    key: str,
477	    out: dict[str, Any],
478	    *,
479	    source_name: str,
480	) -> None:
481	    if key not in section:
482	        return
483	    value = section.get(key)
484	    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int):
485	        raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] {key} must be an integer")
486	    out[key] = int(value)
487	
488	
489	def _assign_bool(
490	    section: Mapping[str, Any],
491	    key: str,
492	    out: dict[str, Any],
493	    *,
494	    source_name: str,
495	) -> None:
496	    if key not in section:
497	        return
498	    value = section.get(key)
499	    if not isinstance(value, bool):
500	        raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] {key} must be a boolean")
501	    out[key] = value
502	
503	
504	def _assign_path(
505	    section: Mapping[str, Any],
506	    key: str,
507	    out: dict[str, Any],
508	    *,
509	    source_name: str,
510	) -> None:
511	    if key not in section:
512	        return
513	    value = section.get(key)
514	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
515	        raise ValueError(f"[{source_name}] {key} must be a non-empty path string")
516	    raw = Path(value.strip()).expanduser()
517	    out[key] = (PROJECT_ROOT / raw).resolve() if not raw.is_absolute() else raw.resolve()
518	
519	
520	def _apply_overrides(target: dict[str, Any], overrides: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
521	    for key, value in overrides.items():
522	        if key == "model_overrides":
523	            existing = target.get("model_overrides")
524	            merged = dict(existing) if isinstance(existing, Mapping) else {}
525	            if isinstance(value, Mapping):
526	                for model_key, model_value in value.items():
527	                    model_text = str(model_value).strip()
528	                    if model_text:
529	                        merged[str(model_key)] = model_text
530	            target["model_overrides"] = merged
531	            continue
532	        target[key] = value
533	
```

    DEFAULT_GRAPH_ID,
    GJOPEN_MAX_PAGES_DEFAULT,
    GJOPEN_PROMPT_REV,
    GJOPEN_START_URL,
    PROJECT_ROOT,
)
from packages.storage.runtime_config import StorageRuntimeConfig

    *,
    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
    paths: RunConfigPaths,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return serialise_effective_settings(settings=settings, paths=paths)


def build_run_research_behavior_config(settings: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    run_research = {key: _json_value(settings.get(key)) for key in RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS if key in settings}
    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "run_research": run_research,
        "tuned_heads": {},
    }


def run_research_settings_from_run_config(config: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    run_research = config.get("run_research") if isinstance(config, Mapping) else {}
    if not isinstance(run_research, Mapping):
        return {}
    return {str(key): _json_value(value) for key, value in run_research.items()}


def build_run_config_execution_snapshot(stored_run_config) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: ANN001
    llm_config = dict(stored_run_config.llm_config)
    snapshot = dict(llm_config)
    snapshot["run_config_id"] = stored_run_config.run_config_id
    snapshot["run_config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
    snapshot["config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
    snapshot["run_config"] = dict(stored_run_config.config)
    snapshot["llm_config"] = llm_config
    return snapshot


def create_run_research_run_config(
    *,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig,
    cli_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
    label: str = "",
    description: str = "",
    source_kind: str = "manual",
    created_by: str | None = None,
):
    from packages.inference.llm import build_llm_config_snapshot
    from packages.storage.run_config_store import create_or_get_run_config

    behavior_overrides = {key: value for key, value in dict(cli_overrides).items() if key in RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS}
    settings, _paths = build_effective_run_research_settings(
        cli_overrides=behavior_overrides,
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )
    llm_config = build_llm_config_snapshot(model_set=str(settings.get("model_set") or "").strip() or None)
    settings["model_set"] = str(llm_config.get("model_set") or settings.get("model_set") or "default")
    return create_or_get_run_config(
        config=build_run_research_behavior_config(settings),
        llm_config=llm_config,
        label=label,
        description=description,
        source_kind=source_kind,
        created_by=created_by,
        storage_config=storage_config,
    )


def load_run_research_run_config(
    *,
    run_config_id: str,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig,
):
    from packages.storage.run_config_store import require_run_config

    return require_run_config(run_config_id=run_config_id, storage_config=storage_config)


def apply_run_config_to_settings(settings: Mapping[str, Any], stored_run_config) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: ANN001
    merged = dict(settings)
    for key in RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS:
        merged.pop(key, None)
    merged.update(run_research_settings_from_run_config(stored_run_config.config))
    merged["run_config_id"] = stored_run_config.run_config_id
    merged["run_config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
    merged["run_config_snapshot"] = build_run_config_execution_snapshot(stored_run_config)
    return merged


def _resolve_path_input(value: Path | None, *, default: Path) -> Path:
    path = value or default
    path = Path(path).expanduser()
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = (PROJECT_ROOT / path).resolve()
    return path

def build_effective_run_research_settings(
    *,
    cli_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
    run_profile_path: Path | None = None,
    system_config_path: Path | None = None,
    storage_config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], RunConfigPaths]:
    paths = resolve_run_config_paths(
        run_profile_path=run_profile_path,
        system_config_path=system_config_path,
    )
    if storage_config is not None:
        from packages.storage.runtime_config_store import load_run_research_defaults

        file_overrides = _coerce_loaded_path_overrides(
            load_run_research_defaults(storage_config=storage_config),
            path_keys=("questions_path", "graphs_dir", "output_root", "local_repo_path"),
        )
    else:
        file_overrides = load_run_research_file_overrides(paths=paths)
    merged = merge_run_research_settings(file_overrides=file_overrides, cli_overrides=cli_overrides)
    return merged, paths


def load_generate_questions_file_overrides(*, paths: RunConfigPaths) -> dict[str, Any]:
    merged: dict[str, Any] = {}
    system_payload = _load_toml_payload(paths.system_config_path)
    profile_payload = _load_toml_payload(paths.run_profile_path)

    for source_name, payload in (
        ("system_defaults", system_payload),
        ("run_profile", profile_payload),
    ):

    return {}


def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(key): _json_value(item) for key, item in dict(value).items()}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, tuple):
        return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
    return value


def _coerce_loaded_path_overrides(payload: Mapping[str, Any], *, path_keys: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    out = dict(payload)
    for key in path_keys:
        value = out.get(key)
        if value is None or isinstance(value, Path):
            continue
        text = str(value).strip()
        if text:
            out[key] = Path(text)
    return out


def _assign_optional_str(
    section: Mapping[str, Any],
    key: str,
    out: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    source_name: str,
) -> None:
    if key not in section:
        return

657	            if isinstance(value, Mapping):
658	                for model_key, model_value in value.items():
659	                    model_text = str(model_value).strip()
660	                    if model_text:
661	                        merged[str(model_key)] = model_text
662	            target["model_overrides"] = merged
663	            continue
664	        target[key] = value
665

  "new_string": "def _apply_overrides(target: dict[str, Any], overrides: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:\n    for key, value in overrides.items():\n        if key == \"model_overrides\":\n            existing = target.get(\"model_overrides\")\n            merged = dict(existing) if isinstance(existing, Mapping) else {}\n            if isinstance(value, Mapping):\n                for model_key, model_value in value.items():\n                    model_text = str(model_value).strip()\n                    if model_text:\n                        merged[str(model_key)] = model_text\n            target[\"model_overrides\"] = merged\n            continue\n        target[key] = value\n\n\ndef _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:\n    if isinstance(value, Path):\n        return str(value)\n    if isinstance(value, Mapping):\n        return {str(key): _json_value(item) for key, item in dict(value).items()}\n    if isinstance(value, list):\n        return [_json_value(item) for item in value]\n    if isinstance(value, tuple):\n        return [_json_value(item) for item in value]\n    return value\n\n\ndef _coerce_loaded_path_overrides(payload: Mapping[str, Any], *, path_keys: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    out = dict(payload)\n    for key in path_keys:\n        value = out.get(key)\n        if value is None or isinstance(value, Path):\n            continue\n        text = str(value).strip()\n        if text:\n            out[key] = Path(text)\n    return out"

1	"""
2	Persistent job queue and artifact registry backed by the storage database.
3	"""
4	
5	from __future__ import annotations
6	
7	import uuid
8	from dataclasses import dataclass
9	from datetime import timedelta
10	from pathlib import Path
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	from sqlalchemy import or_, select
14	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
15	
16	from .database import ArtifactRow, JobEventRow, JobRow, RunRow, RunStatus, StorageDatabase, ensure_database_schema, utc_now
17	from .interfaces import ArtifactRepository
18	from .runtime_config import StorageRuntimeConfig, resolve_storage_runtime_config
19	
20	
21	@dataclass(frozen=True)
22	class ClaimedJob:
23	    job_id: str
24	    job_type: str
25	    graph_id: str
26	    run_id: str
27	    run_config_id: str | None
28	    requested_question_ids: list[str]
29	    request_data: dict[str, Any]
30	
31	
32	@dataclass(frozen=True)
33	class StoredArtifact:
34	    artifact_id: str
35	    job_id: str | None
36	    question_id: str | None
37	    run_id: str | None
38	    kind: str
39	    label: str
40	    content_type: str
41	    object_key: str
42	    size_bytes: int
43	    sha256: str
44	
45	
46	class DatabaseJobQueue:
47	    def __init__(self, *, config: StorageRuntimeConfig, database: StorageDatabase | None = None) -> None:
48	        self._config = resolve_storage_runtime_config(config)
49	        self._database = database or ensure_database_schema(self._config)
50	
51	    def submit_job(
52	        self,
53	        *,
54	        job_type: str,
55	        graph_id: str,
56	        run_id: str,
57	        question_ids: list[str],
58	        request_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
59	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
60	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
61	    ) -> str:
62	        job_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
63	        now = utc_now()
64	        effective_request_data = dict(request_data or {})
65	        if run_config_id:
66	            effective_request_data["run_config_id"] = run_config_id
67	        row = JobRow(
68	            job_id=job_id,
69	            job_type=job_type,
70	            state="queued",
71	            graph_id=graph_id,
72	            run_id=run_id,
73	            run_config_id=run_config_id,
74	            requested_question_ids=list(question_ids),
75	            request_data=effective_request_data or None,
76	            processed_count=0,
77	            total_count=len(question_ids),
78	            submitted_at=now,
79	            created_at=now,
80	            updated_at=now,
81	        )
82	        if idempotency_key:
83	            row.idempotency_key = idempotency_key
84	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
85	            session.add(row)
86	        return job_id
87	
88	    def find_by_idempotency_key(self, key: str) -> JobRow | None:
89	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
90	            return session.scalars(
91	                select(JobRow).where(JobRow.idempotency_key == key).order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.desc()).limit(1)
92	            ).first()
93	
94	    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> JobRow | None:
95	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
96	            return session.get(JobRow, job_id)
97	
98	    def list_active_jobs(self, *, limit: int = 10) -> list[JobRow]:
99	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
100	            return list(
101	                session.scalars(
102	                    select(JobRow).where(JobRow.state.in_(["queued", "running"])).order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.asc()).limit(limit)
103	                ).all()
104	            )
105	
106	    def claim_next_job(self, *, worker_name: str | None = None) -> ClaimedJob | None:
107	        owner = str(worker_name or self._config.queue_worker_name).strip() or "elijah-worker"
108	        now = utc_now()
109	        lease_expires = now + timedelta(seconds=max(1, int(self._config.queue_lease_seconds)))
110	        orphan_cutoff = now - timedelta(seconds=2 * max(1, int(self._config.queue_lease_seconds)))
111	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
112	            stmt = (
113	                select(JobRow)
114	                .where(
115	                    or_(
116	                        JobRow.state == "queued",
117	                        (JobRow.state == "running") & (JobRow.lease_expires_at.is_not(None)) & (JobRow.lease_expires_at <= now),
118	                        # Reclaim orphaned jobs with NULL lease that have been running too long
119	                        (JobRow.state == "running")
120	                        & (JobRow.lease_expires_at.is_(None))
121	                        & (JobRow.started_at.is_not(None))
122	                        & (JobRow.started_at <= orphan_cutoff),
123	                    )
124	                )
125	                .order_by(JobRow.submitted_at.asc())
126	            )
127	            if self._database.dialect_name() != "sqlite":
128	                stmt = stmt.with_for_update(skip_locked=True)
129	            row = session.scalars(stmt.limit(1)).first()
130	            if row is None:
131	                return None
132	            row.state = "running"
133	            row.started_at = row.started_at or now
134	            row.lease_owner = owner
135	            row.lease_expires_at = lease_expires
136	            row.updated_at = now
137	            session.add(
138	                JobEventRow(
139	                    job_id=row.job_id,
140	                    event_type="claimed",
141	                    payload={"worker_name": owner},
142	                    created_at=now,
143	                )
144	            )
145	            return ClaimedJob(
146	                job_id=row.job_id,
147	                job_type=row.job_type,
148	                graph_id=row.graph_id,
149	                run_id=row.run_id,
150	                run_config_id=row.run_config_id,
151	                requested_question_ids=list(row.requested_question_ids or []),
152	                request_data=dict(row.request_data or {}),
153	            )
154	
155	    def update_progress(
156	        self,
157	        *,
158	        job_id: str,
159	        processed_count: int,
160	        total_count: int,
161	        current_question_id: str | None,
162	        current_step: str | None,
163	        state: str | None = None,
164	        event_type: str = "progress",
165	        payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
166	    ) -> None:
167	        now = utc_now()
168	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
169	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
170	            if row is None:
171	                raise KeyError(job_id)
172	            row.processed_count = int(processed_count)
173	            row.total_count = int(total_count)
174	            row.current_question_id = str(current_question_id or "").strip() or None
175	            row.current_step = str(current_step or "").strip() or None
176	            row.state = str(state or row.state)
177	            row.lease_expires_at = now + timedelta(seconds=max(1, int(self._config.queue_lease_seconds)))
178	            row.updated_at = now
179	            session.add(
180	                JobEventRow(
181	                    job_id=job_id,
182	                    event_type=event_type,
183	                    payload=dict(payload or {}),
184	                    created_at=now,
185	                )
186	            )
187	
188	    def complete_job(
189	        self,
190	        *,
191	        job_id: str,
192	        state: str,
193	        result_data: dict[str, Any] | None,
194	        error_code: str | None = None,
195	        error_message: str | None = None,
196	    ) -> None:
197	        now = utc_now()
198	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
199	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
200	            if row is None:
201	                raise KeyError(job_id)
202	            row.state = str(state)
203	            row.result_data = dict(result_data or {}) if result_data is not None else None
204	            row.error_code = str(error_code or "").strip() or None
205	            row.error_message = str(error_message or "").strip() or None
206	            row.finished_at = now
207	            row.current_question_id = None
208	            row.current_step = None
209	            row.lease_owner = None
210	            row.lease_expires_at = None
211	            row.updated_at = now
212	            session.add(
213	                JobEventRow(
214	                    job_id=job_id,
215	                    event_type="completed" if state in {"succeeded", "partial"} else ("cancelled" if state == "cancelled" else "failed"),
216	                    payload={
217	                        "state": state,
218	                        "error_code": row.error_code,
219	                        "error_message": row.error_message,
220	                    },
221	                    created_at=now,
222	                )
223	            )
224	            # SPI-219: Sync RunRow.status so API-submitted runs don't stay stuck
225	            # in RUNNING. The _ensure_run_row helper (called during artifact
226	            # registration) creates the RunRow with RUNNING, but nothing on the
227	            # API job path transitioned it to a terminal state.
228	            _JOB_STATE_TO_RUN_STATUS = {
229	                "succeeded": RunStatus.COMPLETED,
230	                "partial": RunStatus.COMPLETED,
231	                "failed": RunStatus.FAILED,
232	                "cancelled": RunStatus.CANCELLED,
233	            }
234	            run_status = _JOB_STATE_TO_RUN_STATUS.get(state)
235	            if run_status and row.run_id:
236	                run_row = session.get(RunRow, row.run_id)
237	                if run_row is not None:
238	                    run_row.status = run_status
239	                    run_row.finished_at = now
240	                    if row.run_config_id is not None:
241	                        run_row.run_config_id = row.run_config_id
242	
243	    def cancel_job(self, job_id: str) -> str:
244	        """Cancel a job. Returns 'cancelled', 'already_terminal', or 'not_found'."""
245	        terminal_states = {"succeeded", "partial", "failed", "cancelled"}
246	        now = utc_now()
247	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
248	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
249	            if row is None:
250	                return "not_found"
251	            if str(row.state or "") in terminal_states:
252	                return "already_terminal"
253	            row.state = "cancelled"
254	            row.finished_at = now
255	            row.current_question_id = None
256	            row.current_step = None
257	            row.lease_owner = None
258	            row.lease_expires_at = None
259	            row.error_code = "operator_cancelled"
260	            row.error_message = "Job cancelled by user."
261	            row.updated_at = now
262	            session.add(
263	                JobEventRow(
264	                    job_id=job_id,
265	                    event_type="cancelled",
266	                    payload={"state": "cancelled"},
267	                    created_at=now,
268	                )
269	            )
270	        return "cancelled"
271	
272	    def is_cancelled(self, job_id: str) -> bool:
273	        """Check if a job has been cancelled."""
274	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
275	            row = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
276	            return row is not None and str(row.state or "") == "cancelled"
277	
278	    def _ensure_run_row(
279	        self,
280	        session: Session,
281	        *,
282	        run_id: str | None,
283	        question_id: str | None,
284	        job_id: str | None,
285	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
286	    ) -> None:
287	        # ArtifactRow.run_id has an FK to runs.run_id, but submit_job only writes a
288	        # JobRow — nothing on the API submission path populates the runs table. Without
289	        # this helper, every run_summary_json write fails with artifacts_run_id_fkey.
290	        # Resolution order for question_id (required by runs_question_id_fkey):
291	        # explicit arg → JobRow.requested_question_ids[0] → give up and let the
292	        # downstream INSERT fail loudly with the original FK error.
293	        if not run_id:
294	            return
295	        resolved_run_config_id = (run_config_id or "").strip() or None
296	        if not resolved_run_config_id and job_id:
297	            job = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
298	            if job is not None:
299	                resolved_run_config_id = job.run_config_id
300	        existing_run = session.get(RunRow, run_id)
301	        if existing_run is not None:
302	            if resolved_run_config_id is not None and existing_run.run_config_id is None:
303	                existing_run.run_config_id = resolved_run_config_id
304	            return
305	
306	        resolved_qid = (question_id or "").strip()
307	        if not resolved_qid and job_id:
308	            job = session.get(JobRow, job_id)
309	            if job is not None:
310	                resolved_run_config_id = resolved_run_config_id or job.run_config_id
311	                requested = job.requested_question_ids or []
312	                if requested:
313	                    resolved_qid = str(requested[0]).strip()
314	        if not resolved_qid:
315	            return
316	
317	        session.add(
318	            RunRow(
319	                run_id=run_id,
320	                question_id=resolved_qid,
321	                job_id=job_id,
322	                run_config_id=resolved_run_config_id,
323	                status=RunStatus.RUNNING,
324	            )
325	        )
326	        session.flush()
327	
328	    def register_artifact(
329	        self,
330	        *,
331	        job_id: str | None,
332	        question_id: str | None,
333	        run_id: str | None,
334	        kind: str,
335	        label: str,
336	        content_type: str,
337	        object_key: str,
338	        size_bytes: int,
339	        sha256: str,
340	    ) -> StoredArtifact:
341	        artifact_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
342	        now = utc_now()
343	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
344	            self._ensure_run_row(session, run_id=run_id, question_id=question_id, job_id=job_id)
345	            row = ArtifactRow(
346	                artifact_id=artifact_id,
347	                job_id=job_id,
348	                question_id=question_id,
349	                run_id=run_id,
350	                kind=kind,
351	                label=label,
352	                content_type=content_type,
353	                object_key=object_key,
354	                size_bytes=int(size_bytes),
355	                sha256=sha256,
356	                created_at=now,
357	            )
358	            session.add(row)
359	        return StoredArtifact(
360	            artifact_id=artifact_id,
361	            job_id=job_id,
362	            question_id=question_id,
363	            run_id=run_id,
364	            kind=kind,
365	            label=label,
366	            content_type=content_type,
367	            object_key=object_key,
368	            size_bytes=int(size_bytes),
369	            sha256=sha256,
370	        )
371	
372	    def store_and_register_artifact(
373	        self,
374	        *,
375	        artifact_repository: ArtifactRepository,
376	        path: Path,
377	        job_id: str | None,
378	        question_id: str | None,
379	        run_id: str | None,
380	        kind: str,
381	        label: str,
382	        content_type: str,
383	    ) -> StoredArtifact:
384	        uploaded = artifact_repository.ensure_object_copy(Path(path))
385	        return self.register_artifact(
386	            job_id=job_id,
387	            question_id=question_id,
388	            run_id=run_id,
389	            kind=kind,
390	            label=label,
391	            content_type=content_type,
392	            object_key=uploaded.object_key,
393	            size_bytes=uploaded.size_bytes,
394	            sha256=uploaded.sha256,
395	        )
396	
397	    def get_artifact(self, artifact_id: str) -> StoredArtifact | None:
398	        with self._database.session_scope() as session:
399	            row = session.get(ArtifactRow, artifact_id)
400	            if row is None:
401	                return None
402	            return StoredArtifact(
403	                artifact_id=row.artifact_id,
404	                job_id=row.job_id,
405	                question_id=row.question_id,
406	                run_id=row.run_id,
407	                kind=row.kind,
408	                label=row.label,
409	                content_type=row.content_type,
410	                object_key=row.object_key,
411	                size_bytes=row.size_bytes,
412	                sha256=row.sha256,
413	            )
414	
415	
416	def resolve_database_job_queue(
417	    config: StorageRuntimeConfig | None = None,
418	) -> DatabaseJobQueue:
419	    resolved = resolve_storage_runtime_config(config)
420	    database = ensure_database_schema(resolved)
421	    return DatabaseJobQueue(config=resolved, database=database)
422	
423	
424	__all__ = ["ClaimedJob", "DatabaseJobQueue", "StoredArtifact", "resolve_database_job_queue"]

"""Per-user authentication foundation for the elijah API (issue #151 sub-PR 1).

Replaces the single-shared-key model (one ``ELIJAH_API_KEY`` for everyone)
with a multi-user identity registry: each user has a name, an API key,
and optional metadata. The middleware resolves the inbound API key to a
``User`` record and attaches it to ``request.state.user`` so routes /
rate-limiters / cost-cap layers can scope on the resolved identity.

Backward-compat by design: when no ``config/api_users.toml`` is present,
the registry falls back to ``ELIJAH_API_KEY`` (resolved as a single
anonymous user named ``"shared"``). Existing single-key deployments
continue working unchanged; multi-user deployments add the toml file
to enable per-user identity.

Sub-PR 1 scope (this PR):

- Identity infrastructure: ``User`` dataclass, ``UserRegistry`` loader,
  ``get_current_user`` dependency.
- Backward-compat with the existing ``require_api_key`` flow.

Out of scope (separate sub-PRs):

- Per-user rate limit key function (sub-PR 2).
- Per-user cost caps + audit attribution (sub-PR 3).
- ``submitted_by`` propagation onto ``JobRequestContext`` (sub-PR 4).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
import secrets
import tomllib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, Security, status
from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Default location for the multi-user registry. Caller-overrideable via
# ``ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH`` env var so tests / staging can use isolated
# files. The shipped repo carries ``config/api_users.example.toml`` as a
# template; real key files are gitignored.
_DEFAULT_USERS_PATH = Path("config/api_users.toml")
_USERS_PATH_ENV = "ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class User:
    """A resolved API caller identity.

    ``user_id`` is the stable identifier used downstream (rate-limit
    bucket, cost-cap key, audit attribution). ``name`` is human-readable
    for logs / UI. ``role`` is informational today (``"operator"``,
    ``"reviewer"``, ``"admin"``); enforcement happens in later sub-PRs.

    The ``metadata`` map holds user-defined key/value pairs (e.g. team
    affiliation, billing tag). Out of scope for sub-PR 1; included for
    forward compatibility.
    """

    user_id: str
    name: str
    role: str = "operator"
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class UserRegistry:
    """Process-wide map of API key → User. Built once at startup.

    Invariant: API keys are unique per registry. Loading a registry with
    duplicate keys raises ``ValueError`` so the operator notices a
    misconfigured config file before users do.

    The registry also remembers whether it's a *single-user fallback*
    (built from ``ELIJAH_API_KEY`` without a toml file) vs *multi-user*
    (built from ``config/api_users.toml``). Tests / health endpoints
    can branch on this for diagnostic messages.
    """

    by_api_key: dict[str, User]
    fallback_mode: bool

    @property
    def is_empty(self) -> bool:
        return not self.by_api_key

    @property
    def n_users(self) -> int:
        return len({user.user_id for user in self.by_api_key.values()})

    def lookup(self, api_key: str) -> User | None:
        """Return the User for ``api_key``, or None if no match.

        Uses constant-time string comparison via :func:`secrets.compare_digest`
        on every entry to avoid timing-side-channel leaks of valid keys.
        """
        if not api_key:
            return None
        for stored_key, user in self.by_api_key.items():
            try:
                if secrets.compare_digest(stored_key, api_key):
                    return user
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                continue
        return None


def load_user_registry(
    *,
    users_path: Path | None = None,
    fallback_api_key: str | None = None,
    fallback_user_id: str = "shared",
    fallback_user_name: str = "shared",
) -> UserRegistry:
    """Build a :class:`UserRegistry` from the active configuration sources.

    Resolution order (first match wins):

    1. ``users_path`` argument if given.
    2. ``ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH`` env var if set.
    3. ``config/api_users.toml`` if it exists in the repo root.
    4. Fallback to a single-user registry built from ``fallback_api_key``
       (typically the ``ELIJAH_API_KEY`` env var passed by the caller).
    5. Empty registry (auth effectively disabled — same as today's
       no-key dev mode).

    The toml schema:

    .. code-block:: toml

        [[users]]
        user_id = "alice"
        name = "Alice"
        api_key = "sk-..."
        role = "operator"

        [users.metadata]
        team = "policy"

    See ``config/api_users.example.toml`` for the canonical template.
    """
    candidate_path: Path | None = users_path
    if candidate_path is None:
        env_path = os.environ.get(_USERS_PATH_ENV, "").strip()
        if env_path:
            candidate_path = Path(env_path)
    if candidate_path is None:
        if _DEFAULT_USERS_PATH.exists():
            candidate_path = _DEFAULT_USERS_PATH

    if candidate_path is not None and candidate_path.exists():
        try:
            return _load_from_toml(candidate_path)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — operator-facing config error
            logger.warning(
                "Failed to load api_users from %s (%s: %s); falling back to ELIJAH_API_KEY.",
                candidate_path,
                type(exc).__name__,
                exc,
            )

    if fallback_api_key:
        return UserRegistry(
            by_api_key={
                fallback_api_key: User(
                    user_id=fallback_user_id,
                    name=fallback_user_name,
                    role="operator",
                )
            },
            fallback_mode=True,
        )

    return UserRegistry(by_api_key={}, fallback_mode=True)


def _load_from_toml(path: Path) -> UserRegistry:
    """Parse a multi-user config from a toml file."""
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        payload = tomllib.load(f)
    raw_users = payload.get("users") or []
    if not isinstance(raw_users, list):
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: 'users' must be a list of tables")
    by_api_key: dict[str, User] = {}
    seen_user_ids: set[str] = set()
    for idx, entry in enumerate(raw_users):
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: users[{idx}] must be a table")
        api_key = str(entry.get("api_key") or "").strip()
        user_id = str(entry.get("user_id") or "").strip()
        name = str(entry.get("name") or user_id).strip()
        role = str(entry.get("role") or "operator").strip()
        metadata_raw = entry.get("metadata") or {}
        if not api_key:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: users[{idx}] missing 'api_key'")
        if not user_id:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: users[{idx}] missing 'user_id'")
        if api_key in by_api_key:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: duplicate api_key in users[{idx}] (user_id={user_id})")
        if user_id in seen_user_ids:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: duplicate user_id={user_id} in users[{idx}]")
        seen_user_ids.add(user_id)
        metadata: dict[str, Any] = dict(metadata_raw) if isinstance(metadata_raw, dict) else {}
        by_api_key[api_key] = User(
            user_id=user_id,
            name=name,
            role=role,
            metadata=metadata,
        )
    return UserRegistry(by_api_key=by_api_key, fallback_mode=False)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FastAPI dependencies
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


_api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name="X-API-Key", auto_error=False)


def get_user_registry(request: Request) -> UserRegistry:
    """Return the process-wide UserRegistry attached at startup.

    The app is responsible for setting ``app.state.user_registry`` during
    startup. Callers should treat the registry as read-only.
    """
    registry: UserRegistry | None = getattr(request.app.state, "user_registry", None)
    if registry is None:
        # No registry configured → empty registry (auth disabled).
        return UserRegistry(by_api_key={}, fallback_mode=True)
    return registry


def get_current_user(
    request: Request,
    supplied_key: str | None = Security(_api_key_header),
) -> User | None:
    """Resolve the inbound API key to a :class:`User`, or return None.

    - If the registry is empty (no ``ELIJAH_API_KEY``, no toml), auth is
      disabled and this returns None — same as today's dev-mode behaviour.
    - If a key is supplied AND the registry is populated, the key MUST
      resolve to a registered user. Otherwise ``HTTP 401``.
    - The resolved user is also attached to ``request.state.user`` so
      downstream middleware (rate-limit key function, cost cap, audit)
      can read it without re-running the resolution.

    Does NOT raise if the registry is empty + no key is supplied — that
    keeps dev-mode workflows unchanged. Production should always set a
    registry; the empty-registry path is for tests and local dev.
    """
    registry = get_user_registry(request)
    if registry.is_empty:
        # Dev mode: auth disabled. No user attached.
        return None

    if not supplied_key:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail={"code": "unauthorized", "message": "Missing API key."},
        )

    user = registry.lookup(supplied_key)
    if user is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail={"code": "unauthorized", "message": "Invalid API key."},
        )

    # Attach to request state for downstream middleware.
    request.state.user = user
    return user


__all__ = [
    "User",
    "UserRegistry",
    "get_current_user",
    "get_user_registry",
    "load_user_registry",
]
```

1	"""
2	Database-backed job service for API submission, polling, and artifact access.
3	"""
4	
5	from __future__ import annotations
6	
7	import uuid
8	from collections.abc import Callable
9	from dataclasses import dataclass
10	from datetime import UTC, datetime
11	from pathlib import Path
12	from typing import Any
13	
14	from packages.contracts import (
15	    ApiError,
16	    JobAcceptedResponse,
17	    JobProgress,
18	    JobRequestContext,
19	    JobResultSummary,
20	    JobState,
21	    JobStatusResponse,
22	    JobType,
23	    ResearchRunMode,
24	)
25	from packages.storage import (
26	    DatabaseJobQueue,
27	    WorkerStorageAdapters,
28	    resolve_database_job_queue,
29	    resolve_worker_storage_adapters,
30	)
31	
32	from .config import ApiConfig
33	
34	type ResearchJobRunner = Callable[..., dict[str, Any]]
35	
36	
37	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
38	class ResolvedArtifact:
39	    artifact_id: str
40	    path: Path
41	    content_type: str
42	    label: str
43	    kind: str
