46	def _format_timestamp(value: datetime | None) -> str | None:
47	    if value is None:
48	        return None
49	    if value.tzinfo is None:
50	        value = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
51	    return value.astimezone(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
52	
53	
54	def _build_run_id(*, prefix: str = "api") -> str:
55	    timestamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
56	    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
57	    return f"{timestamp}_{prefix}_{suffix}"
58	
59	
60	class JobService:
61	    def __init__(
62	        self,
63	        *,
64	        config: ApiConfig | None = None,
65	        storage_adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters | None = None,
66	        research_job_runner: ResearchJobRunner | None = None,
67	        job_queue: DatabaseJobQueue | None = None,
68	    ) -> None:
69	        self._config = config or ApiConfig()
70	        self._storage_adapters = resolve_worker_storage_adapters(
71	            storage_adapters,
72	            runtime_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
73	        )
74	        self._job_queue = job_queue or resolve_database_job_queue(self._config.storage_runtime)
75	        self._research_job_runner = research_job_runner
76	        self._artifact_cache_root = self._config.storage_runtime.local_workspace_root / "artifact_cache"
77	        self._artifact_cache_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
78	
79	    @property
80	    def config(self) -> ApiConfig:
81	        return self._config
82	
83	    @property
84	    def storage_adapters(self) -> WorkerStorageAdapters:
85	        return self._storage_adapters
86	
87	    @property
88	    def job_queue(self) -> DatabaseJobQueue:
89	        return self._job_queue
90	
91	    def submit_research_job(
92	        self,
93	        *,
94	        question_ids: list[str],
95	        graph_id: str | None = None,
96	        mode: ResearchRunMode | None = None,
97	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
98	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
99	        return self._submit_job(
100	            question_ids=question_ids,
101	            graph_id=graph_id,
102	            request_context=JobRequestContext(mode=mode or ResearchRunMode.FULL_RUN),
103	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
104	        )
105	
106	    def submit_research_step_job(
107	        self,
108	        *,
109	        question_id: str,
110	        step_id: str,
111	        graph_id: str | None = None,
112	        filter_mode: str | None = None,
113	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
114	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
115	        return self._submit_job(
116	            question_ids=[question_id],
117	            graph_id=graph_id,
118	            request_context=JobRequestContext(
119	                mode=ResearchRunMode.SINGLE_STEP,
120	                question_id=str(question_id or "").strip() or None,
121	                step_id=str(step_id or "").strip() or None,
122	                filter_mode=str(filter_mode or "").strip() or None,
123	            ),
124	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
125	        )
126	
127	    def submit_research_batch_step_job(
128	        self,
129	        *,
130	        question_ids: list[str],
131	        step_id: str,
132	        graph_id: str | None = None,
133	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
134	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
135	        return self._submit_job(
136	            question_ids=list(question_ids),
137	            graph_id=graph_id,
138	            request_context=JobRequestContext(
139	                mode=ResearchRunMode.BATCH_STEP,
140	                step_id=str(step_id or "").strip() or None,
141	            ),
142	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
143	        )
144	
145	    def _submit_job(
146	        self,
147	        *,
148	        question_ids: list[str],
149	        graph_id: str | None,
150	        request_context: JobRequestContext,
151	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
152	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
153	        resolved_graph_id = str(graph_id or self._config.default_graph_id).strip() or self._config.default_graph_id
154	
155	        # Idempotency: if a key is provided and a non-failed job with the
156	        # same key already exists, return that job instead of creating a new one.
157	        clean_key = str(idempotency_key or "").strip() or None
158	        if clean_key:
159	            existing = self._job_queue.find_by_idempotency_key(clean_key)
160	            if existing is not None and str(existing.state or "") not in ("failed",):
161	                try:
162	                    state = JobState(str(existing.state or JobState.QUEUED.value))
163	                except ValueError:
164	                    state = JobState.QUEUED
165	                return JobAcceptedResponse(
166	                    job_id=existing.job_id,
167	                    job_type=JobType.RESEARCH,
168	                    state=state,
169	                    status_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{existing.job_id}",
170	                    result_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{existing.job_id}/result",
171	                    request_context=request_context,
172	                )
173	
174	        run_id = _build_run_id(prefix="api")
175	        job_id = self._job_queue.submit_job(
176	            job_type=JobType.RESEARCH.value,
177	            graph_id=resolved_graph_id,
178	            run_id=run_id,
179	            question_ids=list(question_ids),
180	            request_data=request_context.model_dump(mode="json"),
181	            idempotency_key=clean_key,
182	        )
183	        return JobAcceptedResponse(
184	            job_id=job_id,
185	            job_type=JobType.RESEARCH,
186	            state=JobState.QUEUED,
187	            status_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{job_id}",
188	            result_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{job_id}/result",
189	            request_context=request_context,
190	        )
191	
192	    def get_status(self, job_id: str) -> JobStatusResponse | None:
193	        row = self._job_queue.get_job(job_id)
194	        if row is None:
195	            return None
196	        try:
197	            state = JobState(str(row.state or JobState.FAILED.value))
198	        except ValueError:
199	            state = JobState.FAILED
200	        try:
201	            job_type = JobType(str(row.job_type or JobType.RESEARCH.value))
202	        except ValueError:
203	            job_type = JobType.RESEARCH
204	
205	        result_payload = row.result_data if isinstance(row.result_data, dict) else None
206	        result = None
207	        if result_payload is not None:
208	            try:
209	                result = JobResultSummary.model_validate(result_payload)
210	            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
211	                result = None
212	
213	        request_context = None
214	        if isinstance(row.request_data, dict):
215	            try:
216	                request_context = JobRequestContext.model_validate(row.request_data)
217	            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
218	                request_context = None
219	
220	        error = None
221	        if row.error_code or row.error_message:
222	            error = ApiError(
223	                code=str(row.error_code or "job_failed"),
224	                message=str(row.error_message or "Job failed."),
225	            )
226	
227	        return JobStatusResponse(
228	            job_id=row.job_id,
229	            job_type=job_type,
230	            state=state,
231	            submitted_at=_format_timestamp(row.submitted_at) or "",
232	            run_id=str(row.run_id or "").strip() or None,
233	            graph_id=str(row.graph_id or "").strip(),
234	            requested_question_ids=list(row.requested_question_ids or []),
235	            request_context=request_context,
236	            progress=JobProgress(
237	                processed_count=int(row.processed_count or 0),
238	                total_count=int(row.total_count or 0),
239	                current_question_id=str(row.current_question_id or "").strip() or None,
240	                current_step=str(row.current_step or "").strip() or None,
241	                started_at=_format_timestamp(row.started_at),
242	                finished_at=_format_timestamp(row.finished_at),
243	            ),
244	            result=result,
245	            error=error,
246	        )
247	
248	    def cancel_job(self, job_id: str) -> str:
249	        """Cancel a job. Returns 'cancelled', 'already_terminal', or 'not_found'."""
250	        return self._job_queue.cancel_job(job_id)
251	
252	    def get_result(self, job_id: str) -> JobResultSummary | None:
253	        status = self.get_status(job_id)
254	        if status is None:
255	            return None
256	        return status.result
257	
258	    def resolve_artifact(self, artifact_id: str) -> ResolvedArtifact | None:
259	        artifact = self._job_queue.get_artifact(artifact_id)
260	        if artifact is None:
261	            return None
262	        downloader = getattr(self._storage_adapters.artifact_repository, "download_object_to_local", None)
263	        if not callable(downloader):
264	            return None
265	        file_name = Path(artifact.object_key).name or artifact.artifact_id
266	        target_path = self._artifact_cache_root / artifact.artifact_id / file_name
267	        path = downloader(object_key=artifact.object_key, target_path=target_path)
268	        return ResolvedArtifact(
269	            artifact_id=artifact.artifact_id,
270	            path=Path(path),
271	            content_type=artifact.content_type,
272	            label=artifact.label,
273	            kind=artifact.kind,
274	        )
275	
276	    def shutdown(self) -> None:
277	        return None
278	
279	
280	__all__ = ["JobService", "ResearchJobRunner", "ResolvedArtifact"]
281

compatibility_model_set = str(request_context.model_set or \"\").strip()\n        if explicit_run_config_id and compatibility_model_set:\n            stored = load_run_research_run_config(\n                run_config_id=explicit_run_config_id,\n                storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,\n            )\n            stored_model_set = str((stored.llm_config or {}).get(\"model_set\") or \"\").strip()\n            if stored_model_set and stored_model_set != compatibility_model_set:\n                raise ValueError(\"run_config_id cannot be combined with a different model_set\")\n            return stored\n        if explicit_run_config_id:\n            return load_run_research_run_config(\n                run_config_id=explicit_run_config_id,\n                storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,\n            )\n        return create_run_research_run_config(\n            storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,\n            cli_overrides={\"model_set\": compatibility_model_set} if compatibility_model_set else {},\n            label=\"api-default\" if not compatibility_model_set else f\"api-{compatibility_model_set}\",\n            description=\"Default API run config created for a submission without an explicit run_config_id.\",\n            source_kind=\"api_default\",\n            created_by=\"api\",\n        )"
}

```
"""Helpers for carrying immutable run-config evidence through worker jobs."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

from packages.inference.llm import build_llm_config_snapshot


def resolve_run_config_context(
    *,
    request_context: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    run_config_id: str | None,
    run_config_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    model_set: str | None,
    llm_profile: str | None,
) -> tuple[str | None, dict[str, Any], str | None]:
    context = request_context or {}
    snapshot = dict(run_config_snapshot or context.get("run_config_snapshot") or {})
    snapshot_llm_config = snapshot.get("llm_config") or {}
    resolved_id = str(run_config_id or context.get("run_config_id") or "").strip() or None
    resolved_model_set = (
        str(model_set or "").strip()
        or str((snapshot_llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip()
        or str(context.get("model_set") or "").strip()
        or str(context.get("llm_profile") or "").strip()
        or str(llm_profile or "").strip()
        or None
    )
    return resolved_id, snapshot, resolved_model_set


def llm_config_for_run_summary(*, run_config_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None, model_set: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    snapshot = dict(run_config_snapshot or {})
    return dict(snapshot.get("llm_config") or {}) if snapshot else build_llm_config_snapshot(model_set=model_set)


def run_config_summary_fields(
    *,
    run_config_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    run_config_id: str | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    snapshot = dict(run_config_snapshot or {})
    return {
        "run_config_id": run_config_id,
        "run_config_hash": snapshot.get("run_config_hash") or snapshot.get("config_hash"),
        "run_config": dict(snapshot.get("run_config") or {}),
    }
```

}

512	    from packages.contracts.jobs import JobRequestContext, JobResultSummary, QuestionJobResult
513

524	    aggregate_artifacts: list[ArtifactRef] = []

539	                continue
540	            path = Path(raw_path)
541	            if path.is_file():

543

545	        retrieval = stage_outcomes.get("retrieval_validation") if isinstance(stage_outcomes, Mapping) else {}
546	        extraction = stage_outcomes.get("driver_extraction") if isinstance(stage_outcomes, Mapping) else {}
547	        indicators = stage_outcomes.get("indicator_extraction") if isinstance(stage_outcomes, Mapping) else {}
548	        row_summary = row.get("summary") if isinstance(row.get("summary"), Mapping) else {}
549	        question_results.append(

"""Tests for the per-user authentication foundation (issue #151 sub-PR 1).

Covers the registry loading paths, duplicate-detection, the FastAPI
``get_current_user`` dependency behaviour, and the constant-time-comparison
contract. The full per-user rate-limit + cost-cap behaviours land in later
sub-PRs and have their own tests.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from services.api.auth import (
    User,
    UserRegistry,
    get_current_user,
    load_user_registry,
)


def _write_users_toml(path: Path, body: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# UserRegistry primitives
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestUserRegistryPrimitives:
    def test_empty_registry_is_empty_and_n_users_is_zero(self) -> None:
        registry = UserRegistry(by_api_key={}, fallback_mode=True)
        assert registry.is_empty is True
        assert registry.n_users == 0

    def test_lookup_returns_none_for_empty_key(self) -> None:
        registry = UserRegistry(
            by_api_key={"sk-real": User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")},
            fallback_mode=False,
        )
        assert registry.lookup("") is None

    def test_lookup_returns_none_for_unknown_key(self) -> None:
        registry = UserRegistry(
            by_api_key={"sk-real": User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")},
            fallback_mode=False,
        )
        assert registry.lookup("sk-bogus") is None

    def test_lookup_returns_user_for_matching_key(self) -> None:
        alice = User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")
        registry = UserRegistry(by_api_key={"sk-real": alice}, fallback_mode=False)
        assert registry.lookup("sk-real") is alice

    def test_lookup_distinguishes_between_users(self) -> None:
        alice = User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")
        bob = User(user_id="bob", name="Bob")
        registry = UserRegistry(
            by_api_key={"sk-a": alice, "sk-b": bob},
            fallback_mode=False,
        )
        assert registry.lookup("sk-a") is alice
        assert registry.lookup("sk-b") is bob

    def test_n_users_counts_distinct_user_ids(self) -> None:
        # Same user_id, two keys (rotation in progress) → still one user.
        alice = User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")
        registry = UserRegistry(
            by_api_key={"sk-old": alice, "sk-new": alice},
            fallback_mode=False,
        )
        assert registry.n_users == 1


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# load_user_registry — path resolution
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestLoadUserRegistryFallback:
    def test_no_toml_no_env_returns_empty_registry(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
        monkeypatch.delenv("ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH", raising=False)
        registry = load_user_registry(fallback_api_key=None)
        assert registry.is_empty is True
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True

    def test_no_toml_with_fallback_key_builds_single_user(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
        monkeypatch.delenv("ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH", raising=False)
        registry = load_user_registry(fallback_api_key="sk-fallback")
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True
        assert registry.n_users == 1
        user = registry.lookup("sk-fallback")
        assert user is not None
        assert user.user_id == "shared"
        assert user.name == "shared"

    def test_explicit_users_path_argument_loads_toml(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "api_users.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice"
api_key = "sk-alice"
role = "operator"

[[users]]
user_id = "bob"
name = "Bob"
api_key = "sk-bob"
role = "reviewer"
""",
        )
        registry = load_user_registry(users_path=toml_path)
        assert registry.fallback_mode is False
        assert registry.n_users == 2
        alice = registry.lookup("sk-alice")
        bob = registry.lookup("sk-bob")
        assert alice is not None and alice.user_id == "alice"
        assert alice.role == "operator"
        assert bob is not None and bob.user_id == "bob"
        assert bob.role == "reviewer"

    def test_env_var_overrides_default_path(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "alt_users.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice"
api_key = "sk-alice"
""",
        )
        monkeypatch.setenv("ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH", str(toml_path))
        registry = load_user_registry()
        assert registry.fallback_mode is False
        assert registry.n_users == 1

    def test_default_path_picked_up_when_present(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        config_dir = tmp_path / "config"
        config_dir.mkdir()
        toml_path = config_dir / "api_users.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice"
api_key = "sk-alice"
""",
        )
        monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
        monkeypatch.delenv("ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH", raising=False)
        registry = load_user_registry()
        assert registry.fallback_mode is False
        assert registry.n_users == 1

    def test_corrupt_toml_falls_back_to_api_key(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
        caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
    ) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "broken.toml"
        toml_path.write_text("not a valid toml [[[", encoding="utf-8")
        monkeypatch.setenv("ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH", str(toml_path))
        with caplog.at_level("WARNING"):
            registry = load_user_registry(fallback_api_key="sk-fallback")
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True
        assert registry.n_users == 1
        assert any("Failed to load api_users" in m for m in caplog.messages)

    def test_metadata_block_is_preserved(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "with_meta.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice"
api_key = "sk-alice"
role = "operator"

[users.metadata]
team = "research"
billing_tag = "pmtl-001"
""",
        )
        registry = load_user_registry(users_path=toml_path)
        alice = registry.lookup("sk-alice")
        assert alice is not None
        assert alice.metadata == {"team": "research", "billing_tag": "pmtl-001"}


class TestLoadUserRegistryValidation:
    def test_duplicate_api_key_raises(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
    ) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "dup_key.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice"
api_key = "sk-shared"

[[users]]
user_id = "bob"
name = "Bob"
api_key = "sk-shared"
""",
        )
        # Loader catches the validation error and falls back to fallback_api_key.
        with caplog.at_level("WARNING"):
            registry = load_user_registry(
                users_path=toml_path,
                fallback_api_key="sk-fallback",
            )
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True
        assert any("duplicate api_key" in m for m in caplog.messages)

    def test_duplicate_user_id_raises(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
    ) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "dup_uid.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice (one)"
api_key = "sk-1"

[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice (two)"
api_key = "sk-2"
""",
        )
        with caplog.at_level("WARNING"):
            registry = load_user_registry(
                users_path=toml_path,
                fallback_api_key="sk-fallback",
            )
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True
        assert any("duplicate user_id" in m for m in caplog.messages)

    def test_missing_api_key_raises(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
    ) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "missing_key.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice"
""",
        )
        with caplog.at_level("WARNING"):
            registry = load_user_registry(
                users_path=toml_path,
                fallback_api_key="sk-fallback",
            )
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True
        assert any("missing 'api_key'" in m for m in caplog.messages)

    def test_missing_user_id_raises(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
    ) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "missing_uid.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
name = "Alice"
api_key = "sk-alice"
""",
        )
        with caplog.at_level("WARNING"):
            registry = load_user_registry(
                users_path=toml_path,
                fallback_api_key="sk-fallback",
            )
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True
        assert any("missing 'user_id'" in m for m in caplog.messages)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FastAPI dependency wiring
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _build_app(registry: UserRegistry) -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI()
    app.state.user_registry = registry

    @app.get("/whoami")
    def whoami(user: User | None = None):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
        # Using Depends in a closure is awkward here; resolve manually.
        # Tests below construct dependency by hand, so this is a placeholder.
        return {"user_id": user.user_id if user else None}

    return app


class TestGetCurrentUserDependency:
    def test_empty_registry_returns_none_without_key(self) -> None:
        from fastapi import Depends

        app = FastAPI()
        app.state.user_registry = UserRegistry(by_api_key={}, fallback_mode=True)

        @app.get("/protected")
        def protected(user: User | None = Depends(get_current_user)):
            return {"user_id": user.user_id if user else None}

        with TestClient(app) as client:
            resp = client.get("/protected")
            assert resp.status_code == 200
            assert resp.json() == {"user_id": None}

    def test_populated_registry_rejects_missing_key(self) -> None:
        app = FastAPI()
        app.state.user_registry = UserRegistry(
            by_api_key={"sk-alice": User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")},
            fallback_mode=False,
        )

        from fastapi import Depends

        @app.get("/protected")
        def protected(user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):  # type: ignore[assignment]
            return {"user_id": user.user_id}

        with TestClient(app) as client:
            resp = client.get("/protected")
            assert resp.status_code == 401
            assert resp.json()["detail"]["code"] == "unauthorized"

    def test_populated_registry_rejects_invalid_key(self) -> None:
        app = FastAPI()
        app.state.user_registry = UserRegistry(
            by_api_key={"sk-alice": User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")},
            fallback_mode=False,
        )

        from fastapi import Depends

        @app.get("/protected")
        def protected(user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):  # type: ignore[assignment]
            return {"user_id": user.user_id}

        with TestClient(app) as client:
            resp = client.get("/protected", headers={"X-API-Key": "sk-bogus"})
            assert resp.status_code == 401

    def test_populated_registry_accepts_valid_key(self) -> None:
        app = FastAPI()
        alice = User(user_id="alice", name="Alice", role="operator")
        app.state.user_registry = UserRegistry(by_api_key={"sk-alice": alice}, fallback_mode=False)

        from fastapi import Depends

        @app.get("/protected")
        def protected(user: User = Depends(get_current_user)):  # type: ignore[assignment]
            return {"user_id": user.user_id, "role": user.role}

        with TestClient(app) as client:
            resp = client.get("/protected", headers={"X-API-Key": "sk-alice"})
            assert resp.status_code == 200
            assert resp.json() == {"user_id": "alice", "role": "operator"}

    def test_request_state_user_attached_after_resolution(self) -> None:
        # Verify directly (no FastAPI route) that get_current_user attaches
        # the resolved User to request.state.user. Avoids FastAPI's
        # body-parameter-handling for non-Pydantic dataclass return types.
        from fastapi import Request

        alice = User(user_id="alice", name="Alice")
        registry = UserRegistry(by_api_key={"sk-alice": alice}, fallback_mode=False)

        # Mock request: app.state.user_registry resolves, request.state is settable.
        class _State:
            pass

        class _AppState:
            user_registry = registry

        class _App:
            state = _AppState()

        request = Request(
            scope={
                "type": "http",
                "app": _App(),
                "headers": [],
                "path": "/whoami",
                "method": "GET",
            }
        )

        resolved = get_current_user(request, supplied_key="sk-alice")
        assert resolved is alice
        assert request.state.user is alice


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# App-level wiring
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestAppFactoryWiresRegistry:
    def test_create_app_attaches_registry_with_fallback_key(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
        monkeypatch.delenv("ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH", raising=False)

        from services.api import create_app
        from tests.support.api_runtime import seed_repo_state

        config = seed_repo_state(tmp_path, api_key="sk-app-fallback")
        app = create_app(config=config)
        assert hasattr(app.state, "user_registry")
        registry: UserRegistry = app.state.user_registry
        assert registry.is_empty is False
        assert registry.fallback_mode is True
        assert registry.lookup("sk-app-fallback") is not None

    def test_create_app_attaches_multi_user_registry_when_toml_present(
        self,
        tmp_path: Path,
        monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    ) -> None:
        toml_path = tmp_path / "users.toml"
        _write_users_toml(
            toml_path,
            """
[[users]]
user_id = "alice"
name = "Alice"
api_key = "sk-alice-multi"

[[users]]
user_id = "bob"
name = "Bob"
api_key = "sk-bob-multi"
""",
        )
        monkeypatch.setenv("ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH", str(toml_path))

        from services.api import create_app
        from tests.support.api_runtime import seed_repo_state

        config = seed_repo_state(tmp_path, api_key="sk-app-fallback")
        app = create_app(config=config)
        registry: UserRegistry = app.state.user_registry
        assert registry.is_empty is False
        assert registry.fallback_mode is False
        assert registry.n_users == 2
        # Toml takes precedence over the ELIJAH_API_KEY fallback.
        assert registry.lookup("sk-app-fallback") is None
        assert registry.lookup("sk-alice-multi") is not None

def merge_run_research_settings(
    *,
    file_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
    cli_overrides: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    merged = dict(RUN_RESEARCH_DEFAULTS)
    _apply_overrides(merged, file_overrides)
    _apply_overrides(merged, cli_overrides)
    model_set = str(merged.get("model_set") or "").strip()
    merged["model_set"] = model_set or None

    # prefer-local can only be active when a local repo path is set.
    merged["prefer_local_repo"] = bool(merged.get("prefer_local_repo")) and merged.get("local_repo_path") is not None
    return merged


def serialise_effective_settings(
    *,
    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
    paths: RunConfigPaths,
) -> dict[str, Any]:

{
  "command": "gh pr create --base dev --draft --title \"SPI-236: Port API auth, run-config propagation, and worker context\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## What this is\n\nPorts Lane 4 of the SPI-238 porting epic. The API on `dev` currently has bare-bones job submission and a single shared API key. This PR adds three capabilities from dev_sprint:\n\nMulti-user authentication via a TOML-based user registry, replacing the single `ELIJAH_API_KEY` model while preserving backward compatibility. End-to-end run-config propagation so the run configuration (model set, behavior flags) flows from API request through the job queue row into worker dispatch and pipeline execution. Worker context helpers so workers can resolve which config and model set to use for a given job.\n\nFS12 route registration was intentionally deferred — the route file imports `packages.domain.fs12` which does not exist on `dev`. Tracked as SPI-239 under a separate \"Port FS12 Pricing Diagnostics to dev\" epic, with SPI-240 documenting the skip in SPI-238.\n\n## Technical detail\n\n### Summary\n\n9 files changed: 3 new modules (`services/api/auth.py`, `services/worker/run_config_context.py`, `tests/test_api_auth.py`), 6 modified (`core/run_config.py`, `services/api/app.py`, `services/api/job_service.py`, `services/api/worker_dispatch.py`, `services/api/routes/jobs.py`, `services/api/mappers.py`).\n\n### Approach\n\n- **Auth**: New `auth.py` with `User`, `UserRegistry`, TOML loader, and `get_current_user` FastAPI dependency. Routes still use the existing `require_api_key`; the new registry is loaded onto `app.state` for sub-PR 2+ to wire per-user rate limits.\n- **Run-config propagation**: `job_service._resolve_run_config_for_submission()` creates or loads a DB-backed run config, threading `run_config_id`/`run_config_hash`/`model_set` into `JobRequestContext`. `worker_dispatch` loads the snapshot from DB and wraps pipeline calls in `model_set_context`.\n- **core/run_config.py gap**: 3 DB-backed functions (`create_run_research_run_config`, `load_run_research_run_config`, `build_run_config_execution_snapshot`) plus `RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS` were ported as a dependency gap between SPI-225 and this ticket.\n- **mappers.py**: Only the `worker_payload_to_job_result` run_config/llm_config field additions. FS1 `target_set_integrity` changes were excluded (not SPI-236 scope).\n\n### Tests\n\n- `tests/test_api_auth.py` — 24 tests covering UserRegistry primitives, TOML loading/validation, fallback, `get_current_user` dependency, and app factory wiring.\n- `tests/test_job_queue_artifacts.py` — existing regression (identical on both branches, 8 tests).\n- `tests/test_run_detail_api.py` — existing regression (identical on both branches, 6 tests).\n- `tests/test_api_service.py` — existing integration tests (30 tests).\n\n### Verification\n\n- [x] `uv run ruff check .`\n- [x] `uv run ruff format --check .`\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest --ignore=tests/frontend -x -q` (988 passed, 1 pre-existing flaky timing test)\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest tests/test_api_auth.py -q` (24 passed)\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest tests/test_job_queue_artifacts.py tests/test_run_detail_api.py tests/test_api_service.py -q` (44 passed)\n\n### Risks\n\n- `core/run_config.py` changes renamed `model_overrides` → `model_set` in `RUN_RESEARCH_DEFAULTS` and `merge_run_research_settings`. Any code that reads `model_overrides` from merged settings will get `None` instead of `{}`. All current tests pass.\n- `worker_dispatch.py` now passes `model_set`, `run_config_id`, `run_config_snapshot` kwargs to pipeline functions. Pipeline functions must accept `**kwargs` or these specific params. Existing pipelines handle this via `**kwargs`.\n\n### Branch-merge\n\nBranch `port/spi-236-api-auth-run-config-worker-context` based on `dev`. No merge conflicts.\n\n### Out of scope (deliberately)\n\n- `services/api/routes/fs12.py` — blocked on `packages/domain/fs12` not existing on dev (tracked as SPI-239)\n- `services/api/routes/driver_promotion.py` edge type comparison fix (bug fix, not SPI-236)\n- `services/api/mappers.py` `target_set_integrity` changes (FS1 scope)\n- `packages/storage/database.py` Alembic migration infrastructure\n- Route-level auth enforcement with `get_current_user` (sub-PR 2+)\n- `submitted_by` propagation to `JobRequestContext` / `JobRow` (sub-PR 4)\n- SPI-240 documents the FS12 deferral in the SPI-238 epic\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\" --reviewer kungfusaini",

> DEVELOPER

Okay , can you please look and see if the review has any of these bad oatters?  ## Code quality patterns

  ### Anti-patterns — reject on sight

  - **Inline system prompts** — prompts must live in `prompts/` and load via `default_prompt_store().load(...)`.
  Never `_SYSTEM_PROMPT = "..."`.
  - **JSON files as storage** — all persistent state goes through the database via `packages/storage/`. JSON file
  storage is retired.
  - **Defensive string wrapping on comparisons** — `str(x or "").strip().upper() == "CONDITIONAL"` adds nothing over
   `x == "CONDITIONAL"`. Reject it.
  - **Global mutation + lazy imports** — `global _x` inside a function body with an inline import is a scratch
  pattern. Revert it.
  - **Dropped model resolution fallback** — use `model or resolve_role_model("tool")`, not `str(model or
  "").strip()`.

  ### Architectural patterns — always follow

  - LLM tools inherit from `BaseLLMTool` and every `LLMConfig()` must have `task_id=`.
  - Use `resolve_task_model("FS3.task")` for named tasks, `resolve_role_model("tool"|"agent")` for generic
  fallbacks. Never hardcode model names.
  - All stage results use `build_stage_outcome()`. Never return ad-hoc dicts.
  - All graph nodes use `build_node_data()` / `build_origin()` / `build_provenance()`.
  - Direct SQL access is confined to `packages/storage/database.py`. Domain code never touches S

1	"""
2	Worker-dispatch bridge for API job execution.
3	"""
4	
5	from __future__ import annotations
6	
7	import importlib
8	from collections.abc import Callable
9	from pathlib import Path
10	from typing import Any
11	
12	from core.run_config import build_run_config_execution_snapshot, load_run_research_run_config
13	from packages.inference.llm import model_set_context
14	from packages.storage import WorkerStorageAdapters
15	
16	
17	def run_research_worker_job(
18	    *,
19	    questions_path: Path,
20	    graphs_dir: Path,
21	    output_root: Path,
22	    run_id: str,
23	    graph_id: str,
24	    question_ids: list[str],
25	    request_context: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
26	    storage_adapters: WorkerStorageAdapters,
27	    progress_callback: Callable[..., None] | None = None,
28	) -> dict[str, Any]:
29	    resolved_context = dict(request_context or {})
30	    run_config_id = str(resolved_context.get("run_config_id") or "").strip() or None
31	    run_config_snapshot = dict(resolved_context.get("run_config_snapshot") or {})
32	    if run_config_id and not run_config_snapshot:
33	        if storage_adapters.storage_runtime_config is None:
34	            raise ValueError("DB-backed worker dispatch requires storage runtime config to load run_config_id")
35	        stored_run_config = load_run_research_run_config(
36	            run_config_id=run_config_id,
37	            storage_config=storage_adapters.storage_runtime_config,
38	        )
39	        run_config_snapshot = build_run_config_execution_snapshot(stored_run_config)
40	        resolved_context["run_config_snapshot"] = run_config_snapshot
41	        resolved_context["run_config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
42	    snapshot_llm_config = run_config_snapshot.get("llm_config") if isinstance(run_config_snapshot, dict) else {}
43	    mode = str(resolved_context.get("mode") or "").strip().lower()
44	    model_set = (
45	        str((snapshot_llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip()
46	        or str(resolved_context.get("model_set") or resolved_context.get("llm_profile") or "").strip()
47	        or None
48	    )
49	    if model_set:
50	        resolved_context["model_set"] = model_set
51	    if mode in {"single_step", "batch_step"}:
52	        step_pipeline_module = importlib.import_module("services.worker.research_driver_step_pipeline")
53	        run_research_driver_single_step_job = getattr(
54	            step_pipeline_module,
55	            "run_research_driver_single_step_job",
56	        )
57	        with model_set_context(model_set):
58	            return run_research_driver_single_step_job(
59	                questions_path=questions_path,
60	                graphs_dir=graphs_dir,
61	                output_root=output_root,
62	                run_id=run_id,
63	                graph_id=graph_id,
64	                question_ids=question_ids,
65	                request_context=resolved_context,
66	                storage_adapters=storage_adapters,
67	                progress_callback=progress_callback,
68	                model_set=model_set,
69	                run_config_id=run_config_id,
70	                run_config_snapshot=run_config_snapshot,
71	            )
72	
73	    pipeline_module = importlib.import_module("services.worker.research_driver_pipeline")
74	    run_research_driver_over_questions = getattr(pipeline_module, "run_research_driver_over_questions")
75	    with model_set_context(model_set):
76	        return run_research_driver_over_questions(
77	            questions_path=questions_path,
78	            graphs_dir=graphs_dir,
79	            output_root=output_root,
80	            run_id=run_id,
81	            graph_id=graph_id,
82	            question_ids=question_ids,
83	            storage_adapters=storage_adapters,
84	            workflow_mode=str(resolved_context.get("mode") or "").strip() or "batch_full_run",
85	            progress_callback=progress_callback,
86	            model_set=model_set,
87	            run_config_id=run_config_id,
88	            run_config_snapshot=run_config_snapshot,
89	        )
90	
91	
92	__all__ = ["run_research_worker_job"]

56	def _format_timestamp(value: datetime | None) -> str | None:
57	    if value is None:
58	        return None
59	    if value.tzinfo is None:
60	        value = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
61	    return value.astimezone(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
62	
63	
64	def _build_run_id(*, prefix: str = "api") -> str:
65	    timestamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
66	    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
67	    return f"{timestamp}_{prefix}_{suffix}"
68

70	class JobService:
71	    def __init__(
72	        self,
73	        *,
74	        config: ApiConfig | None = None,

83	        )
84	        self._job_queue = job_queue or resolve_database_job_queue(self._config.storage_runtime)

86	        self._artifact_cache_root = self._config.storage_runtime.local_workspace_root / "artifact_cache"
87	        self._artifact_cache_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
88

91	        return self._config

93	    @property

96	
97	    @property

100	
101	    def submit_research_job(
102	        self,
103	        *,
104	        question_ids: list[str],
105	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
106	        graph_id: str | None = None,

126	        step_id: str,
127	        run_config_id: str | None = None,

138	                question_id=str(question_id or "").strip() or None,
139	                step_id=str(step_id or "").strip() or None,
140	                filter_mode=str(filter_mode or "").strip() or None,
141	                run_config_id=str(run_config_id or "").strip() or None,
142	                model_set=str(model_set or "").strip() or None,
143	            ),
144	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
145	        )
146	
147	    def submit_research_batch_step_job(
148	        self,
149	        *,
150	        question_ids: list[str],
151	        step_id: str,
152	        run_config_id: str | None = None,
153	        graph_id: str | None = None,
154	        model_set: str | None = None,
155	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
156	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
157	        return self._submit_job(
158	            question_ids=list(question_ids),
159	            graph_id=graph_id,
160	            request_context=JobRequestContext(
161	                mode=ResearchRunMode.BATCH_STEP,
162	                step_id=str(step_id or "").strip() or None,
163	                run_config_id=str(run_config_id or "").strip() or None,
164	                model_set=str(model_set or "").strip() or None,
165	            ),
166	            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
167	        )
168	
169	    def _submit_job(
170	        self,
171	        *,
172	        question_ids: list[str],
173	        graph_id: str | None,
174	        request_context: JobRequestContext,
175	        idempotency_key: str | None = None,
176	    ) -> JobAcceptedResponse:
177	        resolved_graph_id = str(graph_id or self._config.default_graph_id).strip() or self._config.default_graph_id
178	        resolved_run_config = self._resolve_run_config_for_submission(request_context=request_context)
179	        resolved_model_set = str((resolved_run_config.llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip() or None
180	        resolved_request_context = request_context.model_copy(
181	            update={
182	                "run_config_id": resolved_run_config.run_config_id,
183	                "run_config_hash": resolved_run_config.config_hash,
184	                "model_set": resolved_model_set,
185	            }
186	        )
187	
188	        # Idempotency: if a key is provided and a non-failed job with the
189	        # same key already exists, return that job instead of creating a new one.
190	        clean_key = str(idempotency_key or "").strip() or None
191	        if clean_key:
192	            existing = self._job_queue.find_by_idempotency_key(clean_key)
193	            if existing is not None and str(existing.state or "") not in ("failed",):
194	                try:
195	                    state = JobState(str(existing.state or JobState.QUEUED.value))
196	                except ValueError:
197	                    state = JobState.QUEUED
198	                return JobAcceptedResponse(
199	                    job_id=existing.job_id,
200	                    job_type=JobType.RESEARCH,
201	                    state=state,
202	                    status_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{existing.job_id}",
203	                    result_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{existing.job_id}/result",
204	                    request_context=resolved_request_context,
205	                )
206	
207	        run_id = _build_run_id(prefix="api")
208	        job_id = self._job_queue.submit_job(
209	            job_type=JobType.RESEARCH.value,
210	            graph_id=resolved_graph_id,
211	            run_id=run_id,
212	            question_ids=list(question_ids),
213	            request_data=resolved_request_context.model_dump(mode="json", exclude_none=True),
214	            run_config_id=resolved_run_config.run_config_id,
215	            idempotency_key=clean_key,
216	        )
217	        return JobAcceptedResponse(
218	            job_id=job_id,
219	            job_type=JobType.RESEARCH,
220	            state=JobState.QUEUED,
221	            status_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{job_id}",
222	            result_url=f"{self._config.api_prefix}/jobs/{job_id}/result",
223	            request_context=resolved_request_context,
224	        )
225	
226	    def _resolve_run_config_for_submission(self, *, request_context: JobRequestContext):
227	        explicit_run_config_id = str(request_context.run_config_id or "").strip()
228	        compatibility_model_set = str(request_context.model_set or "").strip()
229	        if explicit_run_config_id and compatibility_model_set:
230	            stored = load_run_research_run_config(
231	                run_config_id=explicit_run_config_id,
232	                storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
233	            )
234	            stored_model_set = str((stored.llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip()
235	            if stored_model_set and stored_model_set != compatibility_model_set:
236	                raise ValueError("run_config_id cannot be combined with a different model_set")
237	            return stored
238	        if explicit_run_config_id:
239	            return load_run_research_run_config(
240	                run_config_id=explicit_run_config_id,
241	                storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
242	            )
243	        return create_run_research_run_config(
244	            storage_config=self._config.storage_runtime,
245	            cli_overrides={"model_set": compatibility_model_set} if compatibility_model_set else {},
246	            label="api-default" if not compatibility_model_set else f"api-{compatibility_model_set}",
247	            description="Default API run config created for a submission without an explicit run_config_id.",
248	            source_kind="api_default",
249	            created_by="api",
250	        )
251	
252	    def get_status(self, job_id: str) -> JobStatusResponse | None:
253	        row = self._job_queue.get_job(job_id)
254	        if row is None:
255	            return None
256	        try:
257	            state = JobState(str(row.state or JobState.FAILED.value))
258	        except ValueError:
259	            state = JobState.FAILED
260	        try:
261	            job_type = JobType(str(row.job_type or JobType.RESEARCH.value))
262	        except ValueError:
263	            job_type = JobType.RESEARCH
264	
265	        result_payload = row.result_data if isinstance(row.result_data, dict) else None
266	        result = None
267	        if result_payload is not None:
268	            try:
269	                result = JobResultSummary.model_validate(result_payload)
270	            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
271	                result = None
272	
273	        request_context = None
274	        if isinstance(row.request_data, dict):
275	            try:
276	                request_context = JobRequestContext.model_validate(row.request_data)
277	            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
278	                request_context = None
279	
280	        error = None
281	        if row.error_code or row.error_message:
282	            error = ApiError(
283	                code=str(row.error_code or "job_failed"),
284	                message=str(row.error_message or "Job failed."),
285	            )
286	
287	        return JobStatusResponse(
288	            job_id=row.job_id,
289	            job_type=job_type,
290	            state=state,
291	            submitted_at=_format_timestamp(row.submitted_at) or "",
292	            run_id=str(row.run_id or "").strip() or None,
293	            graph_id=str(row.graph_id or "").strip(),
294	            requested_question_ids=list(row.requested_question_ids or []),
295	            request_context=request_context,
296	            progress=JobProgress(
297	                processed_count=int(row.processed_count or 0),
298	                total_count=int(row.total_count or 0),
299	                current_question_id=str(row.current_question_id or "").strip() or None,
300	                current_step=str(row.current_step or "").strip() or None,
301	                started_at=_format_timestamp(row.started_at),
302	                finished_at=_format_timestamp(row.finished_at),
303	            ),
304	            result=result,
305	            error=error,
306	        )
307	
308	    def cancel_job(self, job_id: str) -> str:
309	        """Cancel a job. Returns 'cancelled', 'already_terminal', or 'not_found'."""
310	        return self._job_queue.cancel_job(job_id)
311	
312	    def get_result(self, job_id: str) -> JobResultSummary | None:
313	        status = self.get_status(job_id)
314	        if status is None:
315	            return None
316	        return status.result
317	
318	    def resolve_artifact(self, artifact_id: str) -> ResolvedArtifact | None:
319	        artifact = self._job_queue.get_artifact(artifact_id)
320	        if artifact is None:
321	            return None
322	        downloader = getattr(self._storage_adapters.artifact_repository, "download_object_to_local", None)
323	        if not callable(downloader):
324	            return None
325	        file_name = Path(artifact.object_key).name or artifact.artifact_id
326	        target_path = self._artifact_cache_root / artifact.artifact_id / file_name
327	        try:
328	            path = downloader(object_key=artifact.object_key, target_path=target_path)
329	        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
330	            logger.warning(
331	                "Artifact metadata points at missing bytes",
332	                extra={"artifact_id": artifact.artifact_id, "object_key": artifact.object_key},
333	                exc_info=True,
334	            )
335	            raise ArtifactBytesMissingError(artifact_id=artifact.artifact_id, object_key=artifact.object_key) from exc
336	        return ResolvedArtifact(
337	            artifact_id=artifact.artifact_id,
338	            path=Path(path),
339	            content_type=artifact.content_type,
340	            label=artifact.label,
341	            kind=artifact.kind,
342	        )
343	
344	    def shutdown(self) -> None:
345	        return None
346	
347	
348	__all__ = ["ArtifactBytesMissingError", "JobService", "ResearchJobRunner", "ResolvedArtifact"]
349

1	"""Helpers for carrying immutable run-config evidence through worker jobs."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from collections.abc import Mapping
6	from typing import Any
7	
8	from packages.inference.llm import build_llm_config_snapshot
9	
10	
11	def resolve_run_config_context(
12	    *,
13	    request_context: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
14	    run_config_id: str | None,
15	    run_config_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
16	    model_set: str | None,
17	    llm_profile: str | None,
18	) -> tuple[str | None, dict[str, Any], str | None]:
19	    context = request_context or {}
20	    snapshot = dict(run_config_snapshot or context.get("run_config_snapshot") or {})
21	    snapshot_llm_config = snapshot.get("llm_config") or {}
22	    resolved_id = str(run_config_id or context.get("run_config_id") or "").strip() or None
23	    resolved_model_set = (
24	        str(model_set or "").strip()
25	        or str((snapshot_llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip()
26	        or str(context.get("model_set") or "").strip()
27	        or str(context.get("llm_profile") or "").strip()
28	        or str(llm_profile or "").strip()
29	        or None
30	    )
31	    return resolved_id, snapshot, resolved_model_set
32	
33	
34	def llm_config_for_run_summary(*, run_config_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None, model_set: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
35	    snapshot = dict(run_config_snapshot or {})
36	    return dict(snapshot.get("llm_config") or {}) if snapshot else build_llm_config_snapshot(model_set=model_set)
37	
38	
39	def run_config_summary_fields(
40	    *,
41	    run_config_snapshot: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
42	    run_config_id: str | None,
43	) -> dict[str, Any]:
44	    snapshot = dict(run_config_snapshot or {})
45	    return {
46	        "run_config_id": run_config_id,
47	        "run_config_hash": snapshot.get("run_config_hash") or snapshot.get("config_hash"),
48	        "run_config": dict(snapshot.get("run_config") or {}),
49	    }

1	"""Per-user authentication foundation for the elijah API (issue #151 sub-PR 1).
2	
3	Replaces the single-shared-key model (one ``ELIJAH_API_KEY`` for everyone)
4	with a multi-user identity registry: each user has a name, an API key,
5	and optional metadata. The middleware resolves the inbound API key to a
6	``User`` record and attaches it to ``request.state.user`` so routes /
7	rate-limiters / cost-cap layers can scope on the resolved identity.
8	
9	Backward-compat by design: when no ``config/api_users.toml`` is present,
10	the registry falls back to ``ELIJAH_API_KEY`` (resolved as a single
11	anonymous user named ``"shared"``). Existing single-key deployments
12	continue working unchanged; multi-user deployments add the toml file
13	to enable per-user identity.
14	
15	Sub-PR 1 scope (this PR):
16	
17	- Identity infrastructure: ``User`` dataclass, ``UserRegistry`` loader,
18	  ``get_current_user`` dependency.
19	- Backward-compat with the existing ``require_api_key`` flow.
20	
21	Out of scope (separate sub-PRs):
22	
23	- Per-user rate limit key function (sub-PR 2).
24	- Per-user cost caps + audit attribution (sub-PR 3).
25	- ``submitted_by`` propagation onto ``JobRequestContext`` (sub-PR 4).
26	"""
27	
28	from __future__ import annotations
29	
30	import logging
31	import os
32	import secrets
33	import tomllib
34	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
35	from pathlib import Path
36	from typing import Any
37	
38	from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, Security, status
39	from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
40	
41	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
42	
43	# Default location for the multi-user registry. Caller-overrideable via
44	# ``ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH`` env var so tests / staging can use isolated
45	# files. The shipped repo carries ``config/api_users.example.toml`` as a
46	# template; real key files are gitignored.
47	_DEFAULT_USERS_PATH = Path("config/api_users.toml")
48	_USERS_PATH_ENV = "ELIJAH_API_USERS_PATH"
49	
50	
51	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
52	class User:
53	    user_id: str
54	    name: str
55	    role: str = "operator"
56	    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
57	
58	
59	@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
60	class UserRegistry:
61	    by_api_key: dict[str, User]
62	    fallback_mode: bool
63	
64	    @property
65	    def is_empty(self) -> bool:
66	        return not self.by_api_key
67	
68	    @property
69	    def n_users(self) -> int:
70	        return len({user.user_id for user in self.by_api_key.values()})
71	
72	    def lookup(self, api_key: str) -> User | None:
73	        if not api_key:
74	            return None
75	        for stored_key, user in self.by_api_key.items():
76	            try:
77	                if secrets.compare_digest(stored_key, api_key):
78	                    return user
79	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
80	                continue
81	        return None
82	
83	
84	def load_user_registry(
85	    *,
86	    users_path: Path | None = None,
87	    fallback_api_key: str | None = None,
88	    fallback_user_id: str = "shared",
89	    fallback_user_name: str = "shared",
90	) -> UserRegistry:
91	    candidate_path: Path | None = users_path
92	    if candidate_path is None:
93	        env_path = os.environ.get(_USERS_PATH_ENV, "").strip()
94	        if env_path:
95	            candidate_path = Path(env_path)
96	    if candidate_path is None:
97	        if _DEFAULT_USERS_PATH.exists():
98	            candidate_path = _DEFAULT_USERS_PATH
99	
100	    if candidate_path is not None and candidate_path.exists():
101	        try:
102	            return _load_from_toml(candidate_path)
103	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — operator-facing config error
104	            logger.warning(
105	                "Failed to load api_users from %s (%s: %s); falling back to ELIJAH_API_KEY.",
106	                candidate_path,
107	                type(exc).__name__,
108	                exc,
109	            )
110	
111	    if fallback_api_key:
112	        return UserRegistry(
113	            by_api_key={
114	                fallback_api_key: User(
115	                    user_id=fallback_user_id,
116	                    name=fallback_user_name,
117	                    role="operator",
118	                )
119	            },
120	            fallback_mode=True,
121	        )
122	
123	    return UserRegistry(by_api_key={}, fallback_mode=True)
124	
125	
126	def _load_from_toml(path: Path) -> UserRegistry:
127	    with path.open("rb") as f:
128	        payload = tomllib.load(f)
129	    raw_users = payload.get("users") or []
130	    if not isinstance(raw_users, list):
131	        raise ValueError(f"{path}: 'users' must be a list of tables")
132	    by_api_key: dict[str, User] = {}
133	    seen_user_ids: set[str] = set()
134	    for idx, entry in enumerate(raw_users):
135	        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
136	            raise ValueError(f"{path}: users[{idx}] must be a table")
137	        api_key = str(entry.get("api_key") or "").strip()
138	        user_id = str(entry.get("user_id") or "").strip()
139	        name = str(entry.get("name") or user_id).strip()
140	        role = str(entry.get("role") or "operator").strip()
141	        metadata_raw = entry.get("metadata") or {}
142	        if not api_key:
143	            raise ValueError(f"{path}: users[{idx}] missing 'api_key'")
144	        if not user_id:
145	            raise ValueError(f"{path}: users[{idx}] missing 'user_id'")
146	        if api_key in by_api_key:
147	            raise ValueError(f"{path}: duplicate api_key in users[{idx}] (user_id={user_id})")
148	        if user_id in seen_user_ids:
149	            raise ValueError(f"{path}: duplicate user_id={user_id} in users[{idx}]")
150	        seen_user_ids.add(user_id)
151	        metadata: dict[str, Any] = dict(metadata_raw) if isinstance(metadata_raw, dict) else {}
152	        by_api_key[api_key] = User(
153	            user_id=user_id,
154	            name=name,
155	            role=role,
156	            metadata=metadata,
157	        )
158	    return UserRegistry(by_api_key=by_api_key, fallback_mode=False)
159	
160	
161	_api_key_header = APIKeyHeader(name="X-API-Key", auto_error=False)
162	
163	
164	def get_user_registry(request: Request) -> UserRegistry:
165	    registry: UserRegistry | None = getattr(request.app.state, "user_registry", None)
166	    if registry is None:
167	        return UserRegistry(by_api_key={}, fallback_mode=True)
168	    return registry
169	
170	
171	def get_current_user(
172	    request: Request,
173	    supplied_key: str | None = Security(_api_key_header),
174	) -> User | None:
175	    registry = get_user_registry(request)
176	    if registry.is_empty:
177	        return None
178	
179	    if not supplied_key:
180	        raise HTTPException(
181	            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
182	            detail={"code": "unauthorized", "message": "Missing API key."},
183	        )
184	
185	    user = registry.lookup(supplied_key)
186	    if user is None:
187	        raise HTTPException(
188	            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
189	            detail={"code": "unauthorized", "message": "Invalid API key."},
190	        )
191	
192	    request.state.user = user
193	    return user
194	
195	
196	__all__ = [
197	    "User",
198	    "UserRegistry",
199	    "get_current_user",
200	    "get_user_registry",
201	    "load_user_registry",
202	]
203

Here's the full anti-pattern audit across the four new/modified files:

---

**1. Inline system prompts** — None found. ✓

**2. JSON files as storage** — None found. ✓

**3. Global mutation + lazy imports (`global _x`)** — Not present. The `importlib.import_module` calls in `worker_dispatch.py:52-56` and `:73-74` are dynamic dispatch to avoid circular imports, not global mutation. Worth noting as a code smell but not the flagged anti-pattern.

**4. Defensive string wrapping** — Present in `job_service.py` in the typed-parameter submit methods:

```python
# job_service.py:116-117 — run_config_id is already str | None
run_config_id=str(run_config_id or "").strip() or None,
model_set=str(model_set or "").strip() or None,
```

Same pattern repeated in `submit_research_step_job` (lines 139-142) and `submit_research_batch_step_job` (lines 162-164). The parameters are typed as `str | None` from the Pydantic contract — `str()` wrapping is redundant here. In `_load_from_toml` (auth.py lines 137-140) it's defensible because TOML values are untyped.

**5. Dropped model resolution fallback** — Present in `worker_dispatch.py`:

```python
# lines 44-48
model_set = (
    str((snapshot_llm_config or {}).get("model_set") or "").strip()
    or str(resolved_context.get("model_set") or resolved_context.get("llm_profile") or "").strip()
    or None
)
```

When all three sources are absent, `model_set=None` is passed to `model_set_context(None)` and the pipeline's `model_set=None`. If the pipeline/context treats `None` as "use defaults" that's fine, but there's no explicit fallback to a named default (e.g. `resolve_role_model("default")` or equivalent). Whether this is actually a bug depends on whether `model_set_context(None)` is a no-op or silently uses an unintended default.

---

**Summary of actionable items:**

- `job_service.py` lines 116-117, 139-142, 162-164: strip the `str(x or "")` wrapper on already-typed `str | None` params — just use `x.strip() or None if x else None` or restructure the `JobRequestContext` construction to pass directly.
- `worker_dispatch.py` lines 44-48: confirm whether `model_set_context(None)` is a safe no-op. If it needs a non-None default, add the fallback here.

Want me to fix these now, or just note them in the PR body?

> AGENT

Good findings. Here's what the investigation shows:

**`model_set_context(None)` — Not a bug.**

`model_set_context(None)` sets `_ACTIVE_MODEL_SET` to `None`. When the pipeline later calls `resolve_model_set_selection()` (`tiers.py:121`), it has a complete fallback chain:
1. explicit `model_set` arg
2. deprecated `llm_profile` arg  
3. `_ACTIVE_MODEL_SET` context var → `None` here, falls through
4. `LLM_MODEL_SET` env var
5. `LLM_PROFILE` env var
6. `DEFAULT_MODEL_SET_NAME = "default"`

Passing `None` is the correct "use system default" signal. No dropped fallback.

**Defensive `str()` wrapping — Code smell, not a bug.**

The `str(run_config_id or "").strip() or None` pattern in `job_service.py` submit methods (lines 116-117, 139-142, 162-164) — the `str()` call is redundant because the params are already typed `str | None`. However the `.strip() or None` part is genuinely useful: Pydantic accepts `"  "` as a valid `str | None`, so whitespace normalization at the service layer is real work. The `str()` wrapper itself is unnecessary noise.

**`ResearchJobSubmitRequest` has no `model_set` field** — confirming the route→service path (`routes/jobs.py:50-55`) only threads `run_config_id`, never `model_set`. That's intentional: model set is only reachable via a stored `run_config_id`. The `model_set` param on the submit methods exists for programmatic/internal callers only.

**Verdict:** No bugs. The two live issues are:
- Redundant `str()` on already-typed params in `job_service.py` — cosmetic
- `str()` in `_load_from_toml` (auth.py) — fine, it's an untyped TOML boundary

Worth a quick cleanup of `job_service.py` but nothing blocking. Want me to clean up the redundant `str()` wrapping now?

{"body":"## What this is\n\nPorts Lane 4 of the SPI-238 porting epic. The API on `dev` currently has bare-bones job submission and a single shared API key. This PR adds three capabilities from dev_sprint:\n\nMulti-user authentication via a TOML-based user registry, replacing the single `ELIJAH_API_KEY` model while preserving backward compatibility. End-to-end run-config propagation so the run configuration (model set, behavior flags) flows from API request through the job queue row into worker dispatch and pipeline execution. Worker context helpers so workers can resolve which config and model set to use for a given job.\n\nFS12 route registration was intentionally deferred — the route file imports `packages.domain.fs12` which does not exist on `dev`. Tracked as SPI-239 under a separate \"Port FS12 Pricing Diagnostics to dev\" epic, with SPI-240 documenting the skip in SPI-238.\n\n## Technical detail\n\n### Summary\n\n9 files changed: 3 new modules (`services/api/auth.py`, `services/worker/run_config_context.py`, `tests/test_api_auth.py`), 6 modified (`core/run_config.py`, `services/api/app.py`, `services/api/job_service.py`, `services/api/worker_dispatch.py`, `services/api/routes/jobs.py`, `services/api/mappers.py`).\n\n### Approach\n\n- **Auth**: New `auth.py` with `User`, `UserRegistry`, TOML loader, and `get_current_user` FastAPI dependency. FS1 `target_set_integrity` changes were excluded (not SPI-236 scope).\n\n### Tests\n\n- `tests/test_api_auth.py` — 24 tests covering UserRegistry primitives, TOML loading/validation, fallback, `get_current_user` dependency, and app factory wiring.\n- `tests/test_job_queue_artifacts.py` — existing regression (identical on both branches, 8 tests).\n- `tests/test_run_detail_api.py` — existing regression (identical on both branches, 6 tests).\n- `tests/test_api_service.py` — existing integration tests (30 tests).\n\n### Verification\n\n- [x] `uv run ruff check .`\n- [x] `uv run ruff format --check .`\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest --ignore=tests/frontend -x -q` (988 passed, 1 pre-existing flaky timing test)\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest tests/test_api_auth.py -q` (24 passed)\n- [x] `uv run python -m pytest tests/test_job_queue_artifacts.py tests/test_run_detail_api.py tests/test_api_service.py -q` (44 passed)\n\n### Risks\n\n- `core/run_config.py` changes renamed `model_overrides` → `model_set` in `RUN_RESEARCH_DEFAULTS` and `merge_run_research_settings`. Tests still green: **1236 passed, 5 skipped**. PR is ready to promote — all checks pass, anti-pattern audit done, cleanup committed.

---

On the auth question: this PR doesn't conflict with your Cloudflare ZT setup — they operate at different layers.

**Cloudflare ZT** is a network-layer gate: it controls who can reach the server at all. Users prove identity via email → CF issues a session cookie → your browser talks to the app. This PR knows nothing about that layer and doesn't touch it.

**`api_users.toml` / `ELIJAH_API_KEY`** is an API-key layer: it controls who can call the JSON API routes (the `/api/...` endpoints). Today there's one shared `ELIJAH_API_KEY` that gets injected into the HTML as `window.ELIJAH_API_KEY`, and the frontend sends it with every request.

So right now your stack is:
```
User email → CF ZT → server → frontend (HTML + JS)
                               ↓ uses shared ELIJAH_API_KEY
                            /api/... routes
```

After this PR, the behavior in fallback mode is **identical** to before. You only get multi-user identity if you create `config/api_users.toml`. In the testing/demo phase where everyone comes in via CF ZT and you're not issuing per-user API keys, you stay in fallback mode and nothing changes operationally.

When would you actually want the TOML? If you want:
- **Per-user rate limits** (sub-PR 2) — so one heavy user can't crowd others out
- **Per-user cost attribution** (sub-PR 3) — so you can see who's running how much LLM spend
- **Programmatic API access** without CF ZT — e.g. a script or external tool calling the API directly with its own key

For a demo/testing phase with CF ZT already handling access, you can leave it in fallback mode indefinitely and the TOML becomes useful when you start caring about those per-user concerns.

> AGENT

(Background task confirmed exit 0 — matches what was already reported above.)

> DEVELOPER

So are are are you saying that in the front end if you use it for the front end, then they wouldn't have to add a different key, we wouldn't have to add a different key. It's just for the API for which we need to do this, is that correct?

> AGENT

Yes, exactly. The frontend never asks users to type an API key — the key is baked into the HTML at serve time (`window.ELIJAH_API_KEY = "..."`) and the JS sends it automatically. Users just log in via CF ZT and the browser handles the rest transparently.

The `api_users.toml` multi-user auth is only relevant when something outside the browser needs to call the API — like a script, a CI job, or an external integration that doesn't go through CF ZT. In those cases you'd issue that caller its own key in the TOML.

For your current setup (CF ZT + browser frontend), nothing changes and no one has to manage a different key.

> DEVELOPER

Well then what's the point of this API key? I guess in the future we could have it so that based on the email, then we give them a different API key for Elijah? Like for the backend? Is that the kind of idea behind this?

> AGENT

Right, you've identified the gap. The `api_users.toml` key model is really designed for **programmatic callers** (scripts, integrations, CI) — not for browser users who already authenticated via CF ZT.

For the browser case, the natural evolution would be different: CF ZT already gives you the user's email in a request header (`Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email`). A future sub-PR could read that header and map it to a `User` object directly — no separate API key needed. The `User` / `UserRegistry` abstraction this PR adds is exactly the right place to plug that in, since `get_current_user` could check the CF header as a second identity path alongside the API key.

So the architecture would become:

- **Browser users** → identified via CF ZT header → mapped to `User` by email
- **Programmatic callers** → identified via `X-API-Key` → mapped to `User` by TOML

Both paths resolve to the same `User` object, so per-user rate limits and cost tracking (sub-PRs 2 and 3) work the same regardless of how the identity was established.

That's not in this PR — this PR just lays the identity foundation. But yes, that's the right direction.

```json
{
  "title": "Explore CF ZT → per-user Elijah identity bridging",
  "team": "Spire_Elijah",
  "state": "Backlog",
  "assignee": "c7a8f74f-a7ba-41e8-b771-85fc97b42216",
  "priority": 4,
  "description": "## Background\n\nThe API now has a `UserRegistry` foundation (landed in SPI-236 / PR #184) that maps API keys to `User` objects. Right now browser users authenticate via Cloudflare Zero Trust (email → CF session cookie) and the frontend uses a single shared `ELIJAH_API_KEY` injected into the HTML. There is no per-user identity at the Elijah API layer for browser users.\n\n## Problem\n\nBecause all browser users share one API key, per-user rate limits (sub-PR 2) and cost attribution (sub-PR 3) cannot distinguish between them. CF ZT already knows who each user is (email in `Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email` header) — we just don't use it.\n\n## Options to explore\n\n1. **CF ZT header bridging** — read `Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email` in `get_current_user` as a second identity path alongside `X-API-Key`. Map email → `User` via a config (TOML or DB table).\n\n2. **Auto-issued per-user API keys** — when a browser user first signs in via CF ZT, automatically generate and store a personal API key, then inject that key into the HTML instead of the shared key. Each user gets their own key transparently.\n\n3. **CF ZT JWT verification** — verify the full CF Access JWT on the backend for stronger cryptographic identity proof, then derive a `User` from the claims.\n\n## Goal\n\nBoth paths should resolve to the same `User` object so that sub-PRs 2 and 3 (rate limits, cost caps) work identically regardless of whether identity came from CF ZT or a programmatic API key.\n\n## Notes\n\n- `get_current_user` in `services/api/auth.py` is the right extension point\n- This is exploratory — pick the option that fits the demo/testing phase complexity budget\n- No changes to CF ZT config should be required for option 1 or 2\n"
}
```

+    settings, _paths = build_effective_run_research_settings(
+        cli_overrides=behavior_overrides,
+        storage_config=storage_config,
+    )
+    llm_config = build_llm_config_snapshot(model_set=str(settings.get("model_set") or "").strip() or None)
+    settings["model_set"] = str(llm_config.get("model_set") or settings.get("model_set") or "default")
+    return create_or_get_run_config(

+        llm_config=llm_config,

+

+
+
+def apply_run_config_to_settings(settings: Mapping[str, Any], stored_run_config) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: ANN001
+    merged = dict(settings)
+    for key in RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS:
+        merged.pop(key, None)
+    merged.update(run_research_settings_from_run_config(stored_run_config.config))
+    merged["run_config_id"] = stored_run_config.run_config_id
+    merged["run_config_hash"] = stored_run_config.config_hash
+    merged["run_config_snapshot"] = build_run_config_execution_snapshot(stored_run_config)
+    return merged
+

 def _resolve_path_input(value: Path | None, *, default: Path) -> Path:
     path = value or default

+def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
+    if isinstance(value, Path):
+        return str(value)
+    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
+        return {str(key): _json_value(item) for key, item in dict(value).items()}
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
+    if isinstance(value, tuple):
+        return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
+    return value
+
+
+def _coerce_loaded_path_overrides(payload: Mapping[str, Any], *, path_keys: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    out = dict(payload)
+    for key in path_keys:
+        value = out.get(key)
+        if value is None or isinstance(value, Path):
+            continue
+        text = str(value).strip()
+        if text:
+            out[key] = Path(text)
+    return out

> AGENT

There are five distinct things happening in the diff:

---

**1. `model_overrides` → `model_set`**

The old `RUN_RESEARCH_DEFAULTS` had `"model_overrides": {}` — a dict mapping model roles to specific model strings (e.g. `{"tool": "gpt-4o-mini"}`). That was replaced with a single `"model_set": None` string — a named profile like `"claude"` or `"gpt4"` that covers all roles at once. `merge_run_research_settings` was simplified to match: instead of merging and normalizing a dict, it just normalizes the string to `None` if blank.

---

**2. Two new key-set constants**

`RUN_RESEARCH_DESTINATION_KEYS` — the five path/location settings (`graph_id`, `output_root`, etc.). These are "where does the output go."

`RUN_RESEARCH_BEHAVIOR_KEYS` — all the tunable research knobs (chunk sizes, LLM flags, `model_set`, etc.). These are "how does the research run." The separation matters because only behavior keys get stored in a DB-backed run config — destination keys are job-specific and don't belong there.

---

**3. `build_effective_run_research_settings` gets a `storage_config` param**

Previously this function always loaded defaults from TOML files on disk. Now, when `storage_config` is passed, it loads defaults from the database instead (via `load_run_research_defaults`). This is the bridge between the file-based config world and the DB-backed one.

---

**4. Three new DB-backed functions (the gap SPI-225 missed)**

- `create_run_research_run_config` — the main entry point. Builds effective settings, builds an LLM config snapshot, then calls `create_or_get_run_config` in storage. This is what `job_service` calls every time a job is submitted without an explicit `run_config_id`.

- `load_run_research_run_config` — thin wrapper around `require_run_config` in storage, for loading an existing config by ID.

- `build_run_config_execution_snapshot` — takes a stored run config object and flattens it into a dict (`run_config_id`, `config_hash`, `llm_config`, etc.) that gets attached to the job's `request_context` and travels with the job to the worker.

Plus two supporting functions: `build_run_research_behavior_config` (settings dict → DB-ready config dict) and `apply_run_config_to_settings` (inverse — DB config → merged settings dict).

---

**5. Two private helpers**

`_json_value` — recursively converts `Path`, `Mapping`, `list`, `tuple` to JSON-serializable types so configs can be stored in the DB cleanly.

`_coerce_loaded_path_overrides` — the reverse: converts string path values back to `Path` objects after they come back out of the DB.

---

**The overall picture:** before this PR, `core/run_config.py` was entirely file-based (TOML → settings dict). This PR adds the DB-backed counterpart so a run config can be created once, stored, and then retrieved by ID across the job's full lifecycle from submission through worker execution.
